local function scan(parent)
    for _, item in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
        -- We look for Scripts and ModuleScripts
        if item:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then 
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print("PATH: " .. item:GetFullName())
            print("TYPE: " .. item.ClassName)
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print(item.Source)
            print("\n-- END OF SCRIPT --\n")
        end
    end
end

  03:10:08.301  -- ForbiddenJ's Character Anticheat
-- 
-- Curtails teleporting, flying, no-clipping, and fling-attacking by
-- taking away physics control upon server-side detection.
-- 
-- It is not recommended that you tie any ban code here, because this
-- script can occasionally register false-positives from lag.
-- 
-- Also, this anticheat is somewhat lenient. For example, a cheater
-- can still noclip through physics objects that are moving.

PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
Config = require(script.Config)
MovementTracker = require(script.MovementTracker)

InstanceInfoPrefab = script.Prefabs.InstanceInfo
AnticheatBindPrefab = script.Prefabs.AnticheatBind

function ClearCharacterBind(player)
	local bind = script.Binds:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
	if bind ~= nil then
		bind:Destroy()
	end
end
function BindPlayer(player)
	local function BindCharacter(character)
		ClearCharacterBind(player)
		
		local info = InstanceInfoPrefab:Clone()
		info.Name = "MovementAnticheat"
		info.Parent = character
		local bind = AnticheatBindPrefab:Clone()
		bind.Name = player.Name
		bind.TargetCharacter.Value = character
		bind.Disabled = false
		bind.Parent = script.Binds
	end
	
	if player.Character ~= nil then
		BindCharacter(player.Character)
	end
	
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(BindCharacter)
end

-- Start up stuff.
MovementTracker.Start()
if Config.DiagnosticsEnabled then
	script.Prefabs.MovementAnticheatStatsGui.Parent = game:GetService("StarterGui")
end
for i, player in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
	BindPlayer(player)
end
PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Connect(BindPlayer)
PlayersService.PlayerRemoving:Connect(ClearCharacterBind)

  03:10:08.301  RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

Config = require(script.Parent.Parent.Config)
MovementTracker = require(script.Parent.Parent.MovementTracker)

Character = script.TargetCharacter.Value
Player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
Torso = Character.HumanoidRootPart
RootJoint = Torso:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") or Character.LowerTorso.Root
Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
DiagnosticsFolder = Character.MovementAnticheat
OnPunishment = DiagnosticsFolder.OnPunishment
SessionProfile = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("__profiles"):WaitForChild(Player.Name)

LastCFrame = Torso.CFrame
LastGoodCFrame = Torso.CFrame
LastGroundedAltitude = Torso.CFrame.Position.Y
SpeedHeat = 0 -- If any of these hit 1, the player is punished.
FlightHeat = 0 -- As an exception, players must fall fast enough to avoid triggering FlightHeat.
VelocityHeat = 0
IsBeingPunished = false
CriticalCharacterComponents = {
	Humanoid,
	Torso,
	Torso:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") or Character.LowerTorso.Root,
}

-- Returns whether the part recently moved.
function IsPartHot(part)
	assert(typeof(part) == "Instance" and part:IsA("BasePart"))

	return (Config.AllowNoclippingThroughPhysicsObjects and not part:IsGrounded())
		or MovementTracker.GetTimeSinceMovement(part) < Config.PingTolerance
end

-- Returns whether the server has full control of the player's character.
function DoesServerControlAllCharacterParts()
	for i, object in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart") and object:CanSetNetworkOwnership() and (object:GetNetworkOwner() ~= nil or object:GetNetworkOwnershipAuto()) then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

-- Sets the network owner of every part inside the player's character.
function SetNetworkOwner(owner)
	assert(owner == Player or owner == nil)

	for i, object in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
		if object:IsA("BasePart") and object:CanSetNetworkOwnership() then
			object:SetNetworkOwner(owner)
		end
	end
end

-- Attempts to detect which part the character is resting on, if any.
function FindPartBeingStoodOn(nearbyParts)
	local function raycast(p1, p2, dist)
		local direction = p2 - p1
		if direction.Magnitude > (dist or 5) then
			direction = direction.Unit * (dist or 5)
		end
		local ray = Ray.new(p1, direction)
		local part, position, normal = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithWhitelist(ray, nearbyParts, false)
		local pass = part ~= nil and (normal.Y > 0 or part:IsA("TrussPart"))
		return pass, part, position, normal
	end

	-- Do scatter raycasts
	local scatterOffsets = {
		Vector3.new(0, -5, 0),
		Vector3.new(2, -5, 0),
		Vector3.new(-2, -5, 0),
		Vector3.new(0, -5, 2),
		Vector3.new(0, -5, -2),
	}
	for i, offset in ipairs(scatterOffsets) do
		local pass, part2, position, normal = raycast(Torso.Position, Torso.Position + offset, 7)
		if pass then
			return part2
		end
	end

	-- Do special raycasts in case we missed.
	for i, part1 in ipairs(nearbyParts) do
		-- Raycast straight to object's center.
		local pass, part2, position, normal = raycast(Torso.Position, part1.Position)
		if pass then
			return part2
		end

		-- Raycast sideways, then downwards.
		pass, part2, position, normal =
			raycast(Torso.Position, Vector3.new(part1.Position.X, Torso.Position.Y, part1.Position.Z), 4) -- First attempt.
		if pass then
			return part2
		end
		pass, part2, position, normal =
			raycast(position, position + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0)) -- Second attempt.
		if pass then
			return part2
		end

		-- Raycast on each XYZ plane where two others are locked to the object's center.
		for _, d in ipairs({"X", "Y", "Z"}) do
			-- Find the closest Vector3 aligned on the "d" axis.
			local cFrame = part1.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(Torso.CFrame)
			if cFrame[d] < part1.Size[d] / 2 and cFrame[d] > -part1.Size[d] / 2 then
				local intermediateVector = {X = 0, Y = 0, Z = 0}
				intermediateVector[d] = cFrame[d]
				local intermediateCFrame = CFrame.new(intermediateVector.X, intermediateVector.Y, intermediateVector.Z)
				local targetVector = part1.CFrame:ToWorldSpace(intermediateCFrame).Position

				-- Do a raycast.
				pass, part2, position, normal =
					raycast(Torso.Position, targetVector)
				if pass then
					return part2
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Are we inside terrain water?
	local tRegion = Region3.new(Torso.Position - Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), Torso.Position + Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)):ExpandToGrid(4)
	local td1, td2 = workspace.Terrain:ReadVoxels(tRegion, 4)
	for x, xt in ipairs(td1) do
		for y, yt in ipairs(xt) do
			for z, cell in ipairs(yt) do
				if cell == Enum.Material.Water then
					return workspace.Terrain
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

-- Resets the anti-cheat bind.
function Reset()
	LastGoodCFrame = Torso.CFrame
	LastCFrame = Torso.CFrame
	LastGroundedAltitude = Torso.CFrame.Position.Y
	SpeedHeat = 0
	FlightHeat = 0
	VelocityHeat = 0
end

-- Nullifies whatever the character did recently and takes away physics control for a moment.
function Punish(reason)
	if not IsBeingPunished then
		IsBeingPunished = true

		if Config.PunishmentDuration > 0 then
			SetNetworkOwner(nil)
		end
		ResetCharacter()

		warn(string.format("[Character Anticheat] Reverting %s's character for %s.", Player.Name, reason))
		OnPunishment:FireClient(Player, reason)

		if Config.PunishmentDuration > 0 then
			wait(Config.PunishmentDuration)

			if Config.PunishmentDuration > 1 then
				warn(string.format("[Character Anticheat] %s's punishment ended.", Player.Name))
			end
			Reset()
			SetNetworkOwner(Player)
		end

		IsBeingPunished = false
	end
end

-- Reverts the character position to the last location that was not suspicious.
function ResetCharacter()
	Torso.CFrame = LastGoodCFrame
	Torso.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Torso.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
end

local connection
connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if IsBeingPunished then
		if not DoesServerControlAllCharacterParts() then
			-- How did the player get control back?! Take it away again!
			SetNetworkOwner(nil)
			ResetCharacter()
			warn(string.format("[Character Anticheat] %s somehow got physics control back during punishment. Taking it away again.", Player.Name))
		else
			LastCFrame = Torso.CFrame
			LastGoodCFrame = Torso.CFrame
		end

		return
	end

	local suspicious = false
	local p1 = LastCFrame.Position
	local p2 = Torso.CFrame.Position
	local nearbyParts = {workspace.Terrain}
	local rv = Vector3.new(Config.CheckRadius, Config.CheckRadius, Config.CheckRadius) -- Radius Vector
	for i, part in ipairs(workspace:FindPartsInRegion3(Region3.new(p2 - rv, p2 + rv), Character, math.huge)) do
		if part.CanCollide then
			table.insert(nearbyParts, part)
		end
	end

	-- Curtail no-clipping.
	do
		-- Figure out what nearby parts should block the player.
		local noClipList = {}
		for i, part in ipairs(nearbyParts) do
			local size = part.Size
			if part.CanCollide and not IsPartHot(part) and not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Ignored) and not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) then
				table.insert(noClipList, part)
			end
		end

		-- Figure out whether the player no-clipped.
		local ray = Ray.new(p1, p2 - p1)
		local part, position = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithWhitelist(ray, noClipList, true)

		if part ~= nil then
			Punish("no-clipping")
		end
	end

	-- Curtail speed and teleporting.
	if not Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
		local horizontalMagnitude = math.sqrt((p2.X - p1.X) ^ 2 + (p2.Z - p1.Z) ^ 2)
		local fastestNearbyObjectSpeed = 0
		for i, part in ipairs(nearbyParts) do -- Account for other objects that might be making the player fast.
			if not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Ignored) then
				fastestNearbyObjectSpeed = math.max(fastestNearbyObjectSpeed, part.Velocity.Magnitude)
			end
		end
		local toleratedMaxSpeed = (fastestNearbyObjectSpeed + (Humanoid:GetAttribute("Speed") or game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed)) * Config.SpeedToleranceRatio
		SpeedHeat = math.max(0, SpeedHeat + (horizontalMagnitude / toleratedMaxSpeed - deltaTime) / Config.TimeSpan)
		if SpeedHeat > 0.3 then
			suspicious = true
			if SpeedHeat >= 1 then
				Punish("speeding/teleporting")
				return
			end
		end
	end

	-- Curtail flying.
	local isNearObjects = (FindPartBeingStoodOn(nearbyParts) ~= nil)
	if not isNearObjects and not Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
		suspicious = true
		FlightHeat = math.max(0, FlightHeat + ((p2.Y - p1.Y) / Config.FlightHeatCooldownRequiredFallSpeed + deltaTime) / Config.MaxFlyingDuration)

		-- Figure out a maximum jump height
		local jumpHeightTolerance
		if Humanoid.UseJumpPower then
			-- The '* 1.1' part is in case the humanoid has slight jumpieness from Roblox's weird physics.
			jumpHeightTolerance = (Humanoid.JumpPower ^ 2) / (2 * workspace.Gravity) * 1.1
		else
			jumpHeightTolerance = Humanoid.JumpHeight * 1.1
		end

		if FlightHeat >= 1 or p2.Y > LastGroundedAltitude + jumpHeightTolerance + 1 then
			Punish("flying")
			return
		end
	else
		FlightHeat = 0
		LastGroundedAltitude = p2.Y
	end

	-- Curtail excessive velocity cheats. (Note: Only accounts for RotVelocity. May change in the future.)
	VelocityHeat = math.max(0, VelocityHeat + (Torso.RotVelocity.Magnitude / Config.RotVelocityTolerance - deltaTime) / Config.TimeSpan)
	if VelocityHeat > 0.3 and not Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
		suspicious = true
		if VelocityHeat >= 1 then
			Punish("spin-attacking")
			return
		end
	end

	-- Record last position.
	LastCFrame = Torso.CFrame
	if not suspicious then
		LastGoodCFrame = Torso.CFrame
	end

	-- Share stats with clients.
	if Config.DiagnosticsEnabled then
		DiagnosticsFolder.FlightHeat.Value = FlightHeat
		DiagnosticsFolder.SpeedHeat.Value = SpeedHeat
		DiagnosticsFolder.VelocityHeat.Value = VelocityHeat
	end
end)

-- Prevent potential noanticheat cheats.
local characterConnection
local removedConnection
removedConnection = Character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(object)
	if table.find(CriticalCharacterComponents, object) and Humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
		connection:Disconnect()
		removedConnection:Disconnect()
		characterConnection:Disconnect()
		-- The character turned into a zombie! Aaah!
		Humanoid.Health = 0
		Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
	end
end)

characterConnection = Character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
	if parent == nil then
		connection:Disconnect()
		characterConnection:Disconnect()
		removedConnection:Disconnect()
	end
end)

-- Cheaters are not able to trigger this from their side due to the way
-- replication works.
Torso:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CFrame"):Connect(Reset)  -  Editar

function Update()
	local Diagnostics =
		Player.Character and
		Player.Character:FindFirstChild("FJsMovementAnticheat")
	local SpeedHeat = Diagnostics and Diagnostics:FindFirstChild("SpeedHeat")
	local FlightHeat = Diagnostics and Diagnostics:FindFirstChild("FlightHeat")
	local VelocityHeat = Diagnostics and Diagnostics:FindFirstChild("VelocityHeat")
	local function doMeter(meter, progressSource)
		if progressSource ~= nil then
			local progress = math.max(0, math.min(progressSource.Value, 1))
			meter.Bar.Size = UDim2.new(progress, 0, 1, 0)
			meter.TextLabel.Text = math.ceil(progress * 100) .. "%"
		else
			meter.Bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
			meter.TextLabel.Text = "???"
		end
	end
	doMeter(SpeedHeatMeter, SpeedHeat)
	doMeter(FlightHeatMeter, FlightHeat)
	doMeter(VelocityHeatMeter, VelocityHeat)
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(Update)

-- | Configuration                                                |

local config = {}

-- If true, then the heat NumberValues will reflect the values
-- contained in the script.
config.DiagnosticsEnabled = false

-- How long punished players have to wait before they regain
-- physics control. Depending on the false-positive rate, you
-- may want to set this below one second so it behaves like
-- Minecraft's server anticheat, where it simply teleports you
-- back.
config.PunishmentDuration = 0.5

-- An important number used to determine how patient the server is
-- with players. This can be imagined as the amount of time the
-- player's server-side character has to walk through a door that
-- just closed. This is different from lag spike tolerance.
config.PingTolerance = 2

-- The timespan that the anticheat will account for. Lower values
-- means potential cheats will be stopped sooner, but also means
-- lower tolerance for lag spikes.
config.TimeSpan = 0.6

-- I recommend you leave this true when you have moving physics
-- objects, because players could appear to be noclipping even
-- if they're not cheating. For example, from hopping around
-- while inside a moving boat.
config.AllowNoclippingThroughPhysicsObjects = true

-- Roughly how far the script looks for valid nearby objects.
-- Making this larger doesn't affect how the anticheat determines
-- whether a player is standing on something, but making it smaller
-- may cause the script to think the player is not standing on
-- anything, even if they actually are.
config.CheckRadius = 10

-- How many times the walk speed the player is allowed to run
-- before heating up. This also amounts to the distance a player
-- can instantly teleport before this instantly overheats,
-- multiplied by the TimeSpan.
config.SpeedToleranceRatio = 1.1

-- How long a player can float around before the anticheat overheats.
config.MaxFlyingDuration = 1.2

-- How fast a player must fall down to avoid triggering the
-- anti-flight system. I recommend keeping this above 50
-- to curtail gliding exploits.
config.FlightHeatCooldownRequiredFallSpeed = 50

-- The amount of RotVelocity.Magnitude the anticheat will stay cool
-- with. Fling cheats usually create ludicrous velocities, so they
-- often trigger even generous limits.
config.RotVelocityTolerance = 360 * 12

return config

  03:10:08.302  -- Tracks when parts have moved in the workspace.

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local MovementTracker = {}
local private = {}

MovementTracker.TrackedParts = {} -- A table of tables indexed by the parts they represent
MovementTracker.IsActive = false
private.Hooks = {}

-- Starts up the MovementTracker system.
-- Note: The system will fail if the script that called this is eventually destroyed.
function MovementTracker.Start()
	if MovementTracker.IsActive then
		return
	end
	
	for i, object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		private.TryRegisterPart(object)
	end
	
	private.Hooks[1] = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(private.PhysicsStep)
	private.Hooks[2] = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(private.TryRegisterPart)
	private.Hooks[3] = workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(private.TryUnregisterPart)
	
	MovementTracker.IsActive = true
end

-- Stops the MovementTracker system.
function MovementTracker.Stop()
	if not MovementTracker.IsActive then
		return
	end
	
	for part, entry in pairs(MovementTracker.TrackedParts) do
		MovementTracker.TrackedParts[part] = nil
	end
	for i, hook in pairs(private.Hooks) do
		hook:Disconnect()
		private.Hooks[i] = nil
	end
	
	MovementTracker.IsActive = false
end

-- Gets the amount of time since the given part last moved, in seconds.
function MovementTracker.GetTimeSinceMovement(part)
	assert(MovementTracker.IsActive, "MovementTracker must be active to use this function.")
	assert(typeof(part) == "Instance" and part:IsA("BasePart"))
	
	local entry = MovementTracker.TrackedParts[part]
	return elapsedTime() - entry.LastMovementTime
end

function private.PhysicsStep(deltaTime)
	for part, entry in pairs(MovementTracker.TrackedParts) do
		if part.CFrame ~= entry.LastCFrame then
			entry.LastCFrame = part.CFrame
			entry.LastMovementTime = elapsedTime()
		end
	end
end
function private.TryRegisterPart(part)
	if part:IsA("BasePart") then
		-- Register the part for tracking.
		local entry = {
			Part = part,
			LastMovementTime = elapsedTime(),
			LastCFrame = part.CFrame,
		}
		MovementTracker.TrackedParts[part] = entry
	end
end
function private.TryUnregisterPart(part)
	-- Unregister the part from tracking.
	MovementTracker.TrackedParts[part] = nil
end

return MovementTracker

		particles.rain[v] = n
	end
end

local ready = false
local function update(t)
	clockTime = t
	if _G.DN_cycle then
		Lighting:SetMinutesAfterMidnight(t)
		if math.floor(t) % 1 == 0 then
			local a = math.abs(t/720 - 1)
			for k, v in next, Day do
				if type(v) == "number" then
					Lighting[k] = (1-a)*v + a*Night[k]
				elseif typeof(v) == "Color3" then
					Lighting[k] = v:lerp(Night[k], a)
				end
			end

			Ambiences.Day.Volume = (1-a)*0.15 + a*-0.15
			Ambiences.Night.Volume = (1-a)*-0.1 + a*0.15
		end
	else
		Lighting:SetMinutesAfterMidnight(800)
		for k, v in next, Day do
			Lighting[k] = v
		end
		
		Ambiences.Day.Volume = 0.20
		Ambiences.Night.Volume = 0
	end

	ready = true
end

local function getRandomChild(p)
	return p:GetChildren()[math.random(1, #p:GetChildren())]
end

local function changeBrightness(color, brightness)
	local h, s, v = color:ToHSV()
	return Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v * brightness)
end

local lightsEnabled = false
Lighting:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ClockTime"):Connect(function()
	if ready then
		if Lighting.ClockTime >= 17.6 or Lighting.ClockTime <= 6.3 then
			if not lightsEnabled then
				lightsEnabled = true
				for _,v in pairs(workspace.Buildings.Lights:GetDescendants()) do
					if v:IsA("Light") then
						v.Enabled = true
					end
				end
			end
		elseif lightsEnabled then
			lightsEnabled = false
			for _,v in pairs(workspace.Buildings.Lights:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("Light") then
					v.Enabled = false
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local currentWeather = ""
if localTime then
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		update(tick() / 60 % 1440)
	end)
else
	local Diff, Mult = 0, 24 / fullCycle
	local Event = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GameEnvironment")
	Event.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(type, data)
		--[[if type == "serverTime" then
			Diff = data.t - os.clock() + 1 / Mult * Lighting.ClockTime * 60
		else]]
		if type == "weather" then
			currentWeather = data.weather.Type
			local dur = data.transition and data.weather.Transition.Duration or 16e-5
			for prop, value in pairs(data.weather.Clouds) do
				local tweenValue = value
				if typeof(tweenValue) == "table" then
					tweenValue = math.random(value.min, value.max) / value.divide
				end

				TweenService:Create(clouds, TweenInfo.new(dur, data.weather.Transition.EasingStyle), {[prop] = tweenValue}):Play()
			end

			task.wait(dur / 2)
			for prop,value in pairs(data.weather.Lighting) do
				TweenService:Create(Lighting, TweenInfo.new(5), {[prop] = value}):Play()
			end
			for prop,value in pairs(data.weather.Atmosphere) do
				TweenService:Create(Lighting.Atmosphere, TweenInfo.new(5), {[prop] = value}):Play()
			end
			local oldSky = Lighting:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Sky")
			if oldSky then oldSky:Destroy() end
			local newSky = Assets.sky[data.weather.Sky]:Clone()
			newSky.Parent = Lighting
			if data.weather.Type == "Rain" then
				rainGrid.Parent = workspace
				Assets.sounds.light_rain.Volume = 0
				Assets.sounds.light_rain:Play()
				TweenService:Create(Assets.sounds.light_rain, TweenInfo.new(10), {Volume = Assets.sounds.light_rain:GetAttribute("Volume")}):Play()
				for _,v in pairs(particles.rain) do
					if not v:GetAttribute("Rate") then
						v:SetAttribute("Rate", v.Rate)
					end
					v.Rate = 0
					TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(5), {Rate = v:GetAttribute("Rate")}):Play()
					v.Enabled = true
				end

				local splashReady = false
				task.delay(1, function()
					for _,v in pairs(particles.splash) do
						if not v:GetAttribute("Rate") then
							v:SetAttribute("Rate", v.Rate)
						end
						v.Rate = 0
						TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(5), {Rate = v:GetAttribute("Rate")}):Play()
						v.Enabled = true
					end

					splashReady = true
				end)

				local thunderTick, thunder = tick(), math.random(5, 30)
				local moving = false
				local lastCamCF
				local connection
				connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if currentWeather ~= "Rain" then
						for _,v in pairs(particles.rain) do
							TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(5), {Rate = 0}):Play()
						end

						for _,v in pairs(particles.splash) do
							TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(5), {Rate = 0}):Play()
						end

						for _,v in pairs(particles.fog) do
							v.Enabled = false
						end

						TweenService:Create(Assets.sounds.light_rain, TweenInfo.new(10), {Volume = 0}):Play()
						task.delay(5, function()
							if currentWeather ~= "Rain" then
								rainGrid.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
							end
						end)

						connection:Disconnect()
						return
					end
					
					Lighting.Atmosphere.Color = data.weather.Atmosphere.Color:lerp(changeBrightness(data.weather.Atmosphere.Color, 0.45), math.abs(clockTime/720 - 1))
					
					rainGrid.PrimaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(camera.CFrame.p + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0))
					if tick() - thunderTick >= thunder then
						thunder = math.random(30, 200)
						thunderTick = tick()
						local sound = getRandomChild(Assets.sounds.thunder):Clone()
						sound.Parent = script
						sound:Play()
						Debris:AddItem(sound, sound.TimeLength + 1)
					end

					for _,v in pairs(rainGrid:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("Part") then
							local params = RaycastParams.new()
							params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
							params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Characters, workspace.Objects, workspace.Ignored}
							params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

							local origin = v.Position + Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)
							local dir = Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0)
							local cast = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
							if cast then
								if v.Transparency < 1 then
									v.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
								end
								local dist = ((v.Position + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)) - cast.Position).magnitude
								particles.rain[v].Lifetime = NumberRange.new(dist / math.abs(particles.rain[v].Acceleration.magnitude * 0.9))
							else
								particles.rain[v].Lifetime = v.rain:GetAttribute("Lifetime")
								if v.Transparency < 1 then
									v.Color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 151, 75)
								end
							end

							if splashReady then
								local castSplash = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0), params)
								if castSplash then
									if rainGrid.splashParts[v.Name].Transparency < 1 then
										rainGrid.splashParts[v.Name].Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
									end

									local pos = castSplash.Position
									rainGrid.splashParts[v.Name].CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + castSplash.Normal) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90.1), 0, 0)
									local onScreen,_ = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(castSplash.Position)

										v.Enabled = onScreen
									end
									--particles.fog[v].Enabled = onScreen
								else
									if rainGrid.splashParts[v.Name].Transparency < 1 then
										rainGrid.splashParts[v.Name].Color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 151, 75)
									end
									--particles.splash[v].Enabled = false
									for _,v in pairs(particles.splash) do
										v.Enabled = false
									end
									--particles.fog[v].Enabled = false
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if lastCamCF then
						local dist = (lastCamCF.p - camera.CFrame.p).magnitude
						if dist > 0.5 and not moving then
							moving = true
							for _,v in pairs(particles.rain) do
								v.LockedToPart = true
							end
						elseif dist <= 0.5 and moving then
							moving = false
							for _,v in pairs(particles.rain) do
								v.LockedToPart = false
							end
						end
					end

					lastCamCF = camera.CFrame
				end)
			end
		end
	end)

	while true do
		update(workspace:GetServerTimeNow() * Mult % 1440)
		task.wait(1 / 15)
	end

local function isHead(part, character)
	if string.lower(part.Name) == "head" then
		return true
	else
		local weld = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Weld", true)
		if weld then
			if (weld.Part0 and string.lower(weld.Part0.Name) == "head") or (weld.Part1 and string.lower(weld.Part1.Name) == "head") then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

local function emitParticle(name, multiplier, v, dur)
	local n = Storage.Particles[name].Attachment:Clone()
	if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
		n.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		n.WorldPosition = v
	else
		n.Parent = v
	end
	for _,p in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
		if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") * (multiplier or 1))
		elseif p:IsA("Light") then
			p.Enabled = true
			task.delay(p:GetAttribute("Duration"), function()
				p.Enabled = false
			end)
		end
	end
	Debris:AddItem(n, dur or 3)

	return n
end

local function isObstacle(p1, p2, params)
	return workspace:Raycast((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1), (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2) - (typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1), params)
end

local function findSecondaryWeapon(character)
	local tool = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
	return tool and tool:GetAttribute("Arms") and tool:GetAttribute("Secondary")
end

local function createGuiSurface(texture, part, pos, scale, size, config, dur)
	local surfaceFace, width, height, relativeX, relativeY = WorldToGui:WorldPositionToGuiPosition(part, pos)

	if surfaceFace and width and height and relativeX and relativeY then
		local surfaceGui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
		surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * width, scale * height)
		surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
		surfaceGui.Face = surfaceFace

		local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
		surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
		surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

		local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		decal.Image = texture
		decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
		decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		decal.Size = UDim2.new(0, scale * size.x, 0, scale * size.y)
		decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		decal.Position = UDim2.new(relativeX, 0, relativeY, 0)
		for prop,data in pairs(config) do
			decal[prop] = data
		end
		decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

		surfaceGui.Parent = part
		task.delay(dur or 10, function()
			TweenService:Create(decal, TweenInfo.new(1.5), {ImageTransparency = 1}):Play()
			task.wait(3)
			surfaceGui:Destroy()
		end)

		return surfaceGui
	end

	return false
end

function isHumanoid(part)
	if part and part.Parent and part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		return part.Parent
	elseif part and part.Parent and part.Parent ~= workspace then
		return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
	end

	return false
end

local function giveTool(toolName, player)
	for _,tool in pairs(StorageServer.Tools:GetDescendants()) do
		if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.ToolTip == toolName then
			local newTool = tool:Clone()
			newTool.Parent = player:IsA("Player") and player.Backpack or player
			return newTool
		end
	end

	return false
end
Storage.Events.giveTool.OnInvoke = function(...)
	return giveTool(...)
end

local function playerHasActivity(player)
	for _,v in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE:GetChildren()) do
		if v:FindFirstChild(player.Name) then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function toComma(amount)
	if typeof(amount) == "number" then
		local formatted = amount
		while true do  
			formatted, k = string.gsub(formatted, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", '%1,%2')
			if (k==0) then
				break
			end
		end

		return formatted
	end

	return amount
end

local function getWorldOrientedSurface(part: BasePart, normalId: Enum.NormalId): (CFrame, Vector2)
	local cf = part.CFrame
	local rot = cf - cf.Position

	local nObject = Vector3.fromNormalId(normalId)
	local nWorld = rot * nObject

	-- get orthogonal vector by utilizing the order of NormalId enums
	-- i.e. Front.Value is 5 -> (5+1)%6 = 0 -> Right.Value
	local xWorld = rot * Vector3.fromNormalId((normalId.Value + 1) % 6)

	-- get other orthogonal vector
	local zWorld = nWorld:Cross(xWorld)

	-- make them both point "generally down"
	if xWorld.Y > 0 then xWorld = -xWorld end
	if zWorld.Y > 0 then zWorld = -zWorld end

	-- choose the one pointing "more down" one as the z axis for the surface
	if xWorld.Y < zWorld.Y then zWorld = xWorld end

	-- redefine x axis based on that
	xWorld = nWorld:Cross(zWorld)

	local surfaceRot = CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(), xWorld, nWorld, zWorld)

	-- get width of part in direction of x and y
	local sizeInWorldSpace = rot * part.Size
	local sizeInSurfaceSpace = surfaceRot:Inverse() * sizeInWorldSpace

	-- get position on surface
	local surfaceCFrame = surfaceRot + cf.Position + nWorld * math.abs(sizeInSurfaceSpace.Y) / 2

	return surfaceCFrame, Vector2.new(math.abs(sizeInSurfaceSpace.X), math.abs(sizeInSurfaceSpace.Z))
end

local function normalIdFromVector(vector)
	local epsilon = 0.01
	for _, normalId in pairs(Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems()) do
		if vector.unit:Dot(Vector3.FromNormalId(normalId)) > 1 - epsilon then
			return normalId
		end
	end
end

local offsetPos = {
	["Top"] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0);
	["Center"] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0);
	["Right"] = Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0);
	["TopLeft"] = Vector3.new(1, 1, 0);
	["TopRight"] = Vector3.new(-1, 1, 0);
	["RightBottom"] = Vector3.new(-1, -1, 0);
	["Left"] = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0);
	["Bottom"] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0);
	["BottomLeft"] = Vector3.new(1, -1, 0);
}

local offsetPos2 = {
	["Top"] = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0);
	["Center"] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0);
	["Bottom"] = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
}

_G.getGibFromPosition = function(part, origin)
	local dir, dist = nil, math.huge
	if (part.Name == "Head") then
		for index,offset in pairs(offsetPos) do
			local pos = part.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset / 2)
			local d = getDistance(pos.Position, origin)
			if (d < dist) then
				dir, dist = index, d
			end
		end
	else
		for index,offset in pairs(offsetPos2) do
			local pos = part.CFrame + offset
			local d = getDistance(pos.Position, origin)
			if (d < dist) then
				dir, dist = index, d
			end
		end
	end

	return dir, dist
end

_G.createMoneyStash = function(part, amount, cf)
	local newPart = part:Clone()
	local moneyDisplay = Storage.Miscs.moneyDisplay.moneyDisplay:Clone()
	moneyDisplay.Parent = newPart
	moneyDisplay.ui.amount.Text = "£" .. toComma(amount)
	newPart.CFrame = cf
	newPart.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
	newPart.Parent = workspace.Objects.Money
	newPart:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
	return newPart
end

_G.createSound = function(sound, parent, group, dur)
	local newSound = sound:Clone()
	newSound.Name = "__sound"
	if typeof(parent) == "Vector3" then
		local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
		a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		a.WorldPosition = parent
		newSound.Parent = a
		Debris:AddItem(a, newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	else
		newSound.Parent = parent
		Debris:AddItem(newSound, dur or newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	end

	return newSound
end

_G.createFlashSFX = function(sound, softness, distance, tweenTime)
	sound:SetAttribute("FlashSoftness", softness)
	sound:SetAttribute("FlashDistance", distance)
	sound:SetAttribute("FlashTime", tweenTime)
	CollectionService:AddTag(sound, "_flash")
end

Storage.Events.glass.Event:Connect(function(glass, hitPos, velDir)
	if glass:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) and not glass:GetAttribute("Break") then
		glass:SetAttribute("Break", true)
		local transparent = glass.Transparency
		local collide = glass.CanCollide
		glass.Transparency = 1
		glass.CanCollide = false
		glass.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.shatter), glass):Play()
		for _,v in pairs(Storage.Particles.GlassShatterEffects:GetChildren()) do
			local n = v:Clone()
			n.Parent = glass
			n:Emit(n:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
			Debris:AddItem(n, 5)
		end
		Network:FireAllClients("client", "glass", glass, transparent, hitPos, velDir or Vector3.new())
		if glass:GetAttribute("CanRegen") then
			task.wait(glass:GetAttribute("RegenTime"))
			glass:SetAttribute("Health", glass:GetAttribute("MaxHealth"))
			glass:SetAttribute("Break", false)
			glass.Transparency = transparent
			glass.CanCollide = collide
			glass.Parent = workspace.Buildings.Glass
		end
	end
end)

local function loadAnim(animator, id)
	local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
	animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
	return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
	animTrack:Destroy()
end

local flinchAnims = {
	15531359765, -- left
	15531361771 -- right
}

local function flinch(name, target)
	if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		local animator = target.Humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")
		local lower = string.lower(name)
		if lower == "left arm" or lower == "left leg" then
			loadAnim(animator, flinchAnims[1]):Play()
		elseif lower == "right arm" or lower == "right leg" then
			loadAnim(animator, flinchAnims[2]):Play()
		else
			loadAnim(animator, flinchAnims[math.random(#flinchAnims)]):Play()
		end
	end
end

local function isRealNumber(num)
	return (typeof(num) == "number") and (num == num) and (math.abs(num) ~= math.huge)
end

function isInFront(p1, pos)
	local angle = math.acos(p1.CFrame.LookVector:Dot((pos - p1.Position).unit))
	return angle < math.pi / 2
end

local function deepCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in pairs(original) do
		if type(v) == "table" then
			v = deepCopy(v)
		end
		copy[k] = v
	end

	return copy
end

function isUserOwnedGamepass(player, id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if table.find(data.id, tonumber(id)) and player.gamepasses[name].Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

function giveArmor(player, type, tier)
	local character = player.Character
	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if Storage.Models.Armors:FindFirstChild(type) and Storage.Models.Armors[type]:FindFirstChild("TIER_" .. tier) and humanoid and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") and character:FindFirstChild("Armor") and character.Armor:FindFirstChild(type) then
		if not character.Armor[type]:GetAttribute("Equipped") then
			character.Armor[type]:SetAttribute("Equipped", true)
			character.Armor[type]:SetAttribute("MaxHealth", ArmorDatabase[type]["TIER_" .. tier].durability)
			character.Armor[type]:SetAttribute("Health", character.Armor[type]:GetAttribute("MaxHealth"))
			character.Armor[type]:SetAttribute("Tier", tostring(tier))

			for _,v in pairs(Storage.Models.Armors[type]["TIER_" .. tier].Models:GetChildren()) do
				if character:FindFirstChild(v.WeldTo.Value) then
					local model = v:Clone()
					model.Weld.Part0 = character[v.WeldTo.Value]
					model.Parent = character.Armor[type]
				end
			end

			for _,v in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
					v.Parent = character.Bin
				end
			end

			_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.armor_equip_primary, character.Torso):Play()
			_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.armor_equip), character.Torso):Play()

			humanoid:UnequipTools()
			pcall(function() loadAnim(humanoid.Animator, 15531364230):Play() end)
			return true
		elseif character.Armor[type]:GetAttribute("Tier") == tostring(tier) then
			character.Armor[type]:SetAttribute("Health", character.Armor[type]:GetAttribute("MaxHealth"))
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
Storage.Events.giveArmor.Event:Connect(giveArmor)

function getArmorDamage(character, limbName, damage)
	local rawDamage, hasArmor, broke = damage, false, false
	local armorType = ArmorDatabase.LimbsToArmorType[limbName]
	if armorType and character:FindFirstChild("Armor") and character.Armor:FindFirstChild(armorType) and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Equipped") and character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Health") > 0 then
		hasArmor = true
		local tierData = ArmorDatabase[armorType]["TIER_" .. character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Tier")]
		rawDamage *= tierData.rawDamageMultiplier
		local currentArmorHp = character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Health")
		character.Armor[armorType]:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(currentArmorHp - (damage * tierData.damageDealthMultiplier), 0))
		if character.Armor[armorType]:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 and currentArmorHp > 0 then
			broke = true
		end
	end

	return rawDamage, hasArmor, broke
end

_G.burn = function(character, duration, damage, damageTick, attackerInfo, noRagdoll)
	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid then
		humanoid:SetAttribute("BurningDuration", humanoid:GetAttribute("BurningDuration") + duration)
		humanoid:SetAttribute("BurningDamage", humanoid:GetAttribute("BurningDamage") + damage)

		if not humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") then
			local scream
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Burning", true)
			if humanoid.Health > 0 then
				scream = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.scream_burning), character.Head)
				scream.Name = "hurt"
				scream:Play()
				if not noRagdoll then
					Ragdoll.setGlobal(character, "muscles", true, "Burning", nil, math.huge)
				end
			end
			local euphoria = humanoid.Health > 0
			local burnTick = tick()
			while humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") do
				if tick() - burnTick >= humanoid:GetAttribute("BurningDuration") then
					break
				end

				humanoid:takeDamage(humanoid:GetAttribute("BurningDamage"))
				if not humanoid:GetAttribute("Euphoria") then
					flinch("", character)
				end
				if attackerInfo then
					CombatTag:addTag(character, attackerInfo.character, {damage = humanoid:GetAttribute("BurningDamage"), weapon = attackerInfo.weapon})
				end
				if humanoid.Health <= 0 and euphoria then
					euphoria = false
					if not noRagdoll or humanoid:GetAttribute("Euphoria") then
						Ragdoll.setGlobal(character, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
					end
				end
				task.wait(damageTick)
			end

			if humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("BurningDuration", 0)
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Burning", false)

				if not noRagdoll or humanoid:GetAttribute("Euphoria") then
					Ragdoll.setGlobal(character, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
				end
				if scream then
					scream:Destroy()
				end
			end
		elseif not noRagdoll and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Euphoria") and humanoid.Health > 0 then
			Ragdoll.setGlobal(character, "muscles", true, "Burning", nil, math.huge)
		end
	end
end

function findTool(parent, name)
	for _,v in pairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Tool") and v.ToolTip == name then
			return v
		end
	end

	return false
end

_G.explode = function(origin, data, attackerInfo, shakeInfo, explosionEffect, whitelist)
	if not whitelist then
		whitelist = {}
	end

	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
	local crater = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -10, 0), params)
	if crater then
		local size = math.random(1200, 1500) / 100
		createGuiSurface(
			"rbxassetid://2828069358",
			crater.Instance,
			crater.Position,
			128,
			Vector2.new(size, size),
			{
				["ImageTransparency"] = math.random(150, 250) / 1000,
				["Rotation"] = 0,
				["ResampleMode"] = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated,
			},
			16.5
		)

		local smokeEffect = Storage.Particles.ExplosionSmokeEffect.Attachment:Clone()
		smokeEffect.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(origin)
		smokeEffect.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		task.delay(1.5, function()
			smokeEffect.Smoke.Enabled = true
			task.wait(10)
			smokeEffect.Smoke.Enabled = false
			Debris:AddItem(smokeEffect, 5)
		end)

		Network:FireAllClients("client", "grenade_debris", origin)
	end

	Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.explosions), origin)
	emitParticle(explosionEffect or "ExplosionEffect", nil, origin, 10)

	local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
	overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored}
	overlapParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

	local targetHits = {}
	local hitted = {}
	local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, data.radius, overlapParams)
	for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
		local target = isHumanoid(part)
		local player = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
		local pvp = player and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value or not player
		if target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and not table.find(whitelist, target) and not hitted[target] and part.Parent == target and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp and not isObstacle(origin, part, params) then
			hitted[target] = true
			targetHits[#targetHits + 1] = target

			task.defer(function()
				local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
				local dead = false

				local ragdoll = false
				local dist = getDistance(target.Humanoid.RootPart, origin)
				local baseDamage = data.damage
				local damage = data.radius / (dist / 2) * baseDamage

				if damage > 30 or data.forceRagdoll then
					ragdoll = true
				end

				if attackerInfo then
					CombatTag:addTag(target, attackerInfo.character, {damage = damage, weapon = attackerInfo.weapon})
				end
				target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)

				if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
					dead = true
				end

				local parts = {}
				if dead then
					for _,part in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							local params = RaycastParams.new()
							params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {target}
							params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
							params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

							local origin = part.Position + (part.CFrame.LookVector * -1)
							local dir = part.CFrame.LookVector * 2
							local cast = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
							if cast then
								local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(part, cast.Position)
								Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, cast.Instance, damage, gib, (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - origin).unit * 2, cast.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, cast.Normal, dead)
							end

							if math.random(2) == 1 and #parts < 3 then
								parts[#parts + 1] = part
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if ragdoll then
					target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 2.5)
					target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)

					local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
					force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
					force.Velocity = (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - origin).unit * math.clamp(damage / 4, 40, 150) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(30, 45), 0)
					force.Parent = part
					Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
					if part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
						target.Humanoid.RootPart.Velocity = part.Velocity / 2
					end
				end
				emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position)

				if dead then
					task.defer(function()
						for _,part in pairs(parts) do
							if part:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
								part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
								_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), part):Play()
								emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
		elseif target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
			task.wait(1 / 15)
			if attackerInfo then
				CombatTag:addTag(target, attackerInfo.character, {damage = 1000, weapon = attackerInfo.weapon})
			end
			target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(1000)
		elseif not target and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
			Storage.Events.glass:Fire(part, part.Position, (part.Position - origin).unit * 30 + Vector3.new(0, math.random(8, 12), 0))
		end
	end

	table.clear(hitted)
	hitted = nil

	for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local pChar = player.Character
		local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if pHum.Health > 0 and pHum.RootPart and getDistance(pHum.RootPart, origin) <= shakeInfo.maxDist then
			local dist = getDistance(pHum.RootPart, origin)
			Storage.Events.shake:FireClient(player, shakeInfo.springConfig, math.clamp(shakeInfo.strength - dist, shakeInfo.min, shakeInfo.max), shakeInfo.dur, shakeInfo.blur)
		end
	end

	return targetHits
end

local function handleGrenade(player, character, humanoid, tool, dir)
	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[tool.Name])
	local x0 = tool._mod[settings.handle].CFrame + (dir.unit * 2)
	local v0 = (humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") or humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")) and (tool._mod[settings.handle].Position + Vector3.new(0, -10, 0)).unit * 5 or dir.unit * settings.speed + Vector3.new(0, settings.upForce, 0)
	local model = Storage.Models.Projectiles[tool.Name]:Clone()
	--model.PrimaryPart.CFrame = x0
	model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(x0)
	model.Parent = workspace.Ignored
	model.PrimaryPart.Anchored = false
	for _,v in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
		if not v.Anchored then
			v:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
		end
	end
	model.PrimaryPart.Velocity = v0
	model.PrimaryPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 20)
	if settings.type == "molotov" then
		for _,v in pairs(model.Cloth.Bone:GetDescendants()) do
			if v:IsA("Sound") then
				v:Play()
				break
			end
		end

		local ignoreParts = {workspace.Ignored, model}
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreParts
		local lastPos = model.PrimaryPart.Position
		local connection
		local step = 0
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if tick() - step >= 1 / 15 then
				step = tick()

				if model.PrimaryPart then
					local cast = workspace:Raycast(lastPos, model.PrimaryPart.Position - lastPos, params)
					if cast then
						connection:Disconnect()
						task.wait(1 / 15)
						for _,v in pairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
							if v:IsA("BasePart") and cast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
								v.Transparency = 1
							elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
								v.Enabled = false
							end
						end
						Debris:AddItem(model, 5)

						if cast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
							model.PrimaryPart.Anchored = true
							model.PrimaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position)
							model.PrimaryPart.Velocity = Vector3.new()
							Network:FireAllClients("client", "projectile_debris", model.Name, cast.Position, cast.Normal, lastPos)
						else
							model.PrimaryPart.Velocity *= 0.1
							model.PrimaryPart.RotVelocity *= 0.1
							model.PrimaryPart.CanCollide = true
						end

						if cast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
							_G.createSound(Storage.Miscs.Weapons.Molotov.Sounds.Impact, model.PrimaryPart):Play()
							_G.createSound(Storage.Miscs.Weapons.Molotov.Sounds.Explode, model.PrimaryPart):Play()

							for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								local pChar = player.Character
								local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								if pHum.Health > 0 and pHum.RootPart and getDistance(pHum.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart) <= 150 then
									local dist = getDistance(pHum.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart)
									Storage.Events.shake:FireClient(player, {Damping = 1.2}, math.clamp(60 - dist, 5, 30), 0.8, true)
								end
							end

							local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
							overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, workspace.Buildings}
							overlapParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

							local function handleFireHitbox(firePart)
								task.defer(function()
									while firePart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and firePart:GetAttribute("Enabled") do
										local hitted = {}
										local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(firePart.CFrame + (firePart.CFrame.UpVector * 2.5), firePart.Size + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), overlapParams)
										for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
											if part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
												local target = isHumanoid(part)
												local player = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
												local pvp = player and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value or not player
												if target and not hitted[target] and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp then
													hitted[target] = true

													target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnHit", true)
													target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnCount", target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") + 1)
													if target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") <= 1 then
														task.spawn(function()
															while target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") >= 1 do
																task.wait(1.5)
																if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
																	local n = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") - 1
																	target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnCount", math.max(n, 0))
																end
															end
														end)
													end
													local lastBurnCount = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount")
													task.delay(1, function()
														if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and (target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") < lastBurnCount or target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") <= 0) then
															target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnHit", false)
														end
													end)
													target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(10)
													CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = 10, weapon = "Molotov Cocktail"})
													if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and target:FindFirstChild("Head") then
														local hurt = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.light_hurt), target.Head)
														hurt.Name = "hurt"
														hurt:Play()
													end
													if target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") >= 5 then
														task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 5, 0.1, {character = character, weapon = "Molotov Cocktail"}) end)
													end
												end
											end
										end

										table.clear(hitted)
										hitted = nil

										task.wait(math.random(40, 60) / 100)
									end
								end)
							end

							if not isHumanoid(cast.Instance) and (cast.Instance.Material ~= Enum.Material.Glass or not cast.Instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass)) then
								if normalIdFromVector(cast.Normal) ~= Enum.NormalId.Bottom then
									local fireHitbox = Storage.Models.fireHitbox:Clone()
									fireHitbox.CFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position + (cast.Normal * 0.15), cast.Position + (cast.Normal * 2)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
									if not cast.Instance.Anchored then
										local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
										weld.Part0 = cast.Instance
										weld.Part1 = fireHitbox
										weld.Parent = fireHitbox
										fireHitbox.Anchored = false
									end
									local size = normalIdFromVector(cast.Normal) == Enum.NormalId.Top and math.random(12, 15) or 8
									local hitted = {}
									local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(cast.Position + cast.Normal), Vector3.new(size, size, size), overlapParams)
									for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
										if part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
											local target = isHumanoid(part)
											local player = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
											local pvp = player and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value or not player
											if target and not hitted[target] and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp then
												local params = RaycastParams.new()
												params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, fireHitbox, workspace.Characters}
												if not isObstacle(fireHitbox, part, params) then
													hitted[target] = true

													task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 1.5, 0.15, {character = character, weapon = "Molotov Cocktail"}) end)
													ignoreParts[#ignoreParts + 1] = target
													params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreParts
												end
											end
										end
									end

									table.clear(hitted)
									hitted = nil

									fireHitbox.Size = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
									fireHitbox.fire:Play()
									fireHitbox.Parent = workspace.Ignored
									task.delay(15, function()
										fireHitbox:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
										for _,v in pairs(fireHitbox:GetChildren()) do
											if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
												if v.Name == "Smoke1" and v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
													v.TimeScale = 0
													v.Rate = 0
												end
												v.Enabled = false
											elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
												TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(2.5), {Volume = 0}):Play()
											end
										end
										task.wait(5)
										fireHitbox.Smoke1.TimeScale = 1
										Debris:AddItem(fireHitbox, 3)
									end)
									TweenService:Create(fireHitbox, TweenInfo.new(5), {Size = Vector3.new(size, 0.001, size)}):Play()
									handleFireHitbox(fireHitbox)
								end
							elseif cast.Instance.Material ~= Enum.Material.Glass or not cast.Instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
								local target = isHumanoid(cast.Instance)
								task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 1.5, 0.1, {character = character, weapon = "Molotov Cocktail"}) end)
								ignoreParts[#ignoreParts + 1] = target
								params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreParts
							elseif cast.Instance.Material == Enum.Material.Glass and cast.Instance:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
								_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.glass_hit), cast.Instance):Play()
								cast.Instance:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(cast.Instance:GetAttribute("Health") - cast.Instance:GetAttribute("MaxHealth"), 0))
								if cast.Instance:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
									Storage.Events.glass:Fire(cast.Instance, cast.Position, (cast.Instance.CFrame.p - character.Head.CFrame.p).unit * math.random(30, 40))
								end
							end

							local n = emitParticle("FireExplosionEffect", nil, cast.Position)
							n.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(cast.Position, cast.Position + cast.Normal) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)

							if normalIdFromVector(cast.Normal) ~= Enum.NormalId.Top then
								for k = 1, math.random(4, 6) do
									local flame_cast = workspace:Raycast(cast.Position + cast.Normal, Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 500) / 100, -30, math.random(-500, 500) / 100), params)
									if flame_cast and flame_cast.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
										if not isHumanoid(flame_cast.Instance) then
											local fireHitbox = Storage.Models.fireHitbox:Clone()
											fireHitbox.CFrame = CFrame.new(flame_cast.Position + (flame_cast.Normal * 0.15), flame_cast.Position + (flame_cast.Normal * 2)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
											if not flame_cast.Instance.Anchored then
												local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
												weld.Part0 = flame_cast.Instance
												weld.Part1 = fireHitbox
												weld.Parent = fireHitbox
												fireHitbox.Anchored = false
											end
											local size = math.random(8, 15)
											fireHitbox.Size = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
											fireHitbox.fire:Play()
											fireHitbox.Parent = workspace.Ignored
											task.delay(12, function()
												fireHitbox:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
												for _,v in pairs(fireHitbox:GetChildren()) do
													if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
														if v.Name == "Smoke1" and v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
															v.TimeScale = 0
															v.Rate = 0
														end
														v.Enabled = false
													elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
														TweenService:Create(v, TweenInfo.new(2.5), {Volume = 0}):Play()
													end
												end
												task.wait(5)
												fireHitbox.Smoke1.TimeScale = 1
												Debris:AddItem(fireHitbox, 3)
											end)
											TweenService:Create(fireHitbox, TweenInfo.new(5), {Size = Vector3.new(size, 0.001, size)}):Play()
											handleFireHitbox(fireHitbox)
										else
											local target = isHumanoid(flame_cast.Instance)
											task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 1.5, 0.1, {character = character, weapon = "Molotov Cocktail"}) end)
										end

										local n = emitParticle("FireExplosionEffect", nil, flame_cast.Position)
										n.WorldCFrame = CFrame.new(flame_cast.Position, flame_cast.Position + flame_cast.Normal) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
									end
									task.wait(1 / 10)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					lastPos = model.PrimaryPart.Position
				else
					connection:Disconnect()
					model:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	elseif settings.type == "frag_grenade" then
		Network:FireAllClients("client", "grenade_impact", model)
		local forceParts = {"Latch", "Pin", "Pinloop"}
		for _,v in pairs(forceParts) do
			model[v].Velocity = dir.unit * 30
			model[v].RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 50)
		end
		local ignoreParts = {workspace.Ignored, model, character}
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreParts
		local lastPos = model.PrimaryPart.Position
		local connection
		local step = 0
		local bounce = 0
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if tick() - step >= 1 / 15 then
				step = tick()

				if model.PrimaryPart then
					if not model.PrimaryPart.CanCollide then
						local cast = workspace:Raycast(model.PrimaryPart.Position, model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.unit * 15 + Vector3.new(0, -5, 0), params)
						if cast then
							model.PrimaryPart.CanCollide = true
						end
					end

					local cast = workspace:Raycast(model.PrimaryPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -1.5, 0), params)
					if cast then
						bounce += 1
						if bounce > settings.maxBounce then
							connection:Disconnect()
							model.PrimaryPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.x / 2, model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.y, model.PrimaryPart.Velocity.z / 2)
							model.PrimaryPart.RotVelocity /= 2
							local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
							force.Velocity = Vector3.new()
							force.Parent = model.PrimaryPart

							task.wait(0.8)
							for _,v in pairs(model.PrimaryPart.Blip:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0))
								end
							end
							task.wait(0.5)
							for _,v in pairs(model.PrimaryPart.Blip:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									v.Enabled = false
								end
							end
							model.Handle.Transparency = 1
							model.PrimaryPart.Anchored = true
							model.PrimaryPart.CanCollide = false
							model.PrimaryPart.Velocity = Vector3.new()
							model.PrimaryPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.new()
							task.defer(function() _G.explode(model.PrimaryPart.Position, {damage = settings.damage, radius = settings.radius}, {character = character, weapon = tool.Name}, settings.explosionConfig) end)
							task.wait(5)
							model:Destroy()
							return
						end
					end

					lastPos = model.PrimaryPart.Position
				else
					connection:Disconnect()
					model:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	tool:SetAttribute("Dropped", true)
	for _,v in pairs(tool._mod:GetDescendants()) do
		if v:IsA("BasePart") then
			v.Transparency = 1
		elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
			v:Destroy()
		elseif v:IsA("Light") then
			v.Brightness = 0
		end
	end
	task.wait(1 / 2)
	tool.Parent = workspace.Ignored
	Debris:AddItem(tool, 3)
end

local function fire(character, tool, player, dir)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid.Health > 0 and typeof(tool) == "Instance" and tool:IsA("Tool") and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[tool.Name])
		--Storage.Events.client:Fire("fire", tool.Name, character.Head.Position, mouse.UnitRay.Direction, character, handle, 2500, true)
		if tool._data.magCurrent.Value > 0 and (not player or tick() - playersEventRate[player].fire >= (settings.rate / 2)) and settings.gunType ~= "diy_ft" then
			if player then
				Network:FireOtherClients(player, "client", "fire", tool.Name, tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.WorldPosition, dir, character, tool._mod.Handle)
			else
				Network:FireAllClients("client", "fire", tool.Name, tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.WorldPosition, dir, character, tool._mod.Handle)
			end
		end
		if player then
			playersEventRate[player].fire = tick()
		end
		tool:SetAttribute("BulletHit", 0)

		if tool._data.magCurrent.Value > 0 then
			tool:SetAttribute("LastAmmo", tool._data.magCurrent.Value)
		end
		tool._data.magCurrent.Value -= 1
		if tool._data.magCurrent.Value < 1 then
			if settings.gunType == "rpg" and tool._mod:FindFirstChild("Rocket") then
				for _,v in pairs(tool._mod.Rocket:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("BasePart") then
						v.Transparency = 1
					end
				end
			elseif settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
				tool:SetAttribute("Dropped", true)
				task.delay(1, function()
					if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
						humanoid:UnequipTools()
						task.wait(1 / 30)
					end
					tool.Motor.Part0 = nil
					tool.Motor.Enabled = false
					tool._mod.Handle.Can.Enabled = false
					tool.Parent = workspace.Ignored
					for _,v in pairs(tool._mod:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") then
							if v.Name == "Hitbox" then
								v:Destroy()
							else
								v.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
								v.CanCollide = true
								if v:CanSetNetworkOwnership() then
									v:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
								end
							end
						end
					end
					Debris:AddItem(tool, 3)
				end)

				return
			end
		end

		if settings.gunType == "rpg" and tool._mod:FindFirstChild("Rocket") then
			local rocket = Storage.Models.Rocket:Clone()
			rocket.Transparency = 0
			rocket.Main.CFrame = CFrame.new(tool._mod.Rocket.CFrame.Position, tool._mod.Rocket.CFrame.Position + (dir * 2))
			rocket.Parent = workspace.Ignored
			rocket.Main:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
			for _,v in pairs(rocket.Attachment:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
					v.Enabled = true
				end
			end
			rocket.flying:Play()
			rocket.launch:Play()

			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
			force.Velocity = dir * 150
			force.Parent = rocket.Main

			--[[local gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
			gyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
			gyro.CFrame = rocket.CFrame
			gyro.Parent = rocket.Main]]

			local start = tick()
			local lastPos = rocket.Main.Position

			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, character}

			local originCF = rocket.Main.CFrame
			local degree = 0
			local exploded = false
			while tick() - start < 10 do
				local cast = workspace:Raycast(lastPos, rocket.Main.Position - lastPos, params)
				if cast then
					exploded = true
					rocket.Main.Anchored = true
					rocket.Transparency = 1
					for _,v in pairs(rocket.Attachment:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
							v.Enabled = false
						end
					end
					rocket.flying:Stop()
					rocket.launch:Stop()
					Debris:AddItem(rocket, 3)

					Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.explosions), cast.Position)

					local params = RaycastParams.new()
					params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
					params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
					local crater = workspace:Raycast(cast.Position + cast.Normal, dir * 100, params)
					if crater then
						local size = math.random(1500, 2000) / 100
						createGuiSurface(
							"rbxassetid://2828069358",
							crater.Instance,
							crater.Position,
							128,
							Vector2.new(size, size),
							{
								["ImageTransparency"] = math.random(450, 750) / 1000,
								["Rotation"] = 0,
								["ResampleMode"] = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated,
							},
							10
						)
					end

					for _,player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
						local pChar = player.Character
						local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
						if pHum.Health > 0 and pHum.RootPart and getDistance(pHum.RootPart, cast.Position) <= 250 then
							local dist = getDistance(pHum.RootPart, cast.Position)
							Storage.Events.shake:FireClient(player, {Damping = 1.2}, math.clamp(150 - dist, 20, 120), 1.5, true)
						end
					end

					local explosion = Instance.new("Explosion")
					explosion.BlastPressure = 0
					explosion.BlastRadius = 30
					explosion.ExplosionType = Enum.ExplosionType.NoCraters
					explosion.DestroyJointRadiusPercent = 0
					explosion.Position = cast.Position
					explosion.Parent = workspace.Terrain

					local targets = {}
					local params = OverlapParams.new()
					params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {tool}
					local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(cast.Position, 30, params)
					for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
						local target = isHumanoid(part)
						local targetPlayer = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
						local pvp = targetPlayer and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[targetPlayer.Name].pvp.Value or not targetPlayer
						if target and not targets[target] and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp and (not targetPlayer or targetPlayer ~= player) then
							targets[target] = true

							task.defer(function()
								local parts = {}
								if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
									for _,part in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
										if part:IsA("BasePart") then
											parts[#parts + 1] = part

											if math.random(5) == 1 then
												break
											end
										end
									end
								end

								CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = 150, weapon = tool.Name})
								target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(150)
								target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 3.5)
								target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)

								local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
								force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
								force.Velocity = (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - cast.Position).unit * math.random(30, 40) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(30, 40), 0)
								force.Parent = part
								Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
								part.RotVelocity = part.Velocity * 2

								task.defer(function()
									for _,part in pairs(parts) do
										part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
										if part:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
											part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
											_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), part):Play()
											_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.gore_ripped), part):Play()
											emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)
										end
									end
								end)
							end)
						elseif target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
							task.wait(1 / 15)
							if target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
								CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = 1000, weapon = tool.Name})
								target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(1000)
							end
						elseif not target and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
							Storage.Events.glass:Fire(part, part.Position, (part.Position - cast.Position).unit * 35 + Vector3.new(0, math.random(8, 12), 0))
						end
					end

					local connection
					connection = explosion.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
						if parent == nil then
							connection:Disconnect()
							table.clear(targets)
						end
					end)

					break
				end

				lastPos = rocket.Main.Position
				task.wait(1 / 15)
			end

			if not exploded then
				rocket:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end

local wallCheckParams = RaycastParams.new()
wallCheckParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
wallCheckParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
wallCheckParams.IgnoreWater = true
wallCheckParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
local function getWallsPierced(head, hitPart, walls, ignoreList)
	if not walls then
		walls = 0
	end
	if not ignoreList then
		ignoreList = {workspace.Ignored, workspace.Buildings.Objects, workspace.Characters, workspace.Terrain, workspace.Objects}
	end

	local wallCheckParams = RaycastParams.new()
	wallCheckParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignoreList
	wallCheckParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
	wallCheckParams.IgnoreWater = true
	wallCheckParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
	local wallCheck = workspace:Raycast(head.CFrame.Position, hitPart.CFrame.Position - head.CFrame.Position, wallCheckParams)
	if wallCheck then
		ignoreList[#ignoreList + 1] = wallCheck.Instance
		walls += 1
		return getWallsPierced(head, hitPart, walls, ignoreList)
	end

	return walls
end

local function damage(player, target, tool, hitPart, vel, result, dir)
	local character = player:IsA("Player") and player.Character or player
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid.Health > 0 and (not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") or findSecondaryWeapon(character)) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Carried") and typeof(tool) == "Instance" and tool:IsA("Tool") and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and Storage.Modules.Weapons:FindFirstChild(tool.Name) and hitPart:IsDescendantOf(target) and tool._data.magCurrent.Value >= 0 and tool._data.reload.reloading.Value == false and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and not playerHasActivity(player) --[[and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)]] then
		if ((Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[target.Name].pvp.Value == false) or (player:IsA("Player") and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == false)) and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
			local text = ""
			if player:IsA("Player") then
				if ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == false then
					text = "YOU HAVE PVP MODE OFF"
				elseif Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[target.Name].pvp.Value == false then
					text = "TARGET HAS PVP MODE OFF"
				end
			end

			return false, nil, nil, {
				prop = {
					Text = text,
					TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 94, 97),
				},
				err = true,
				dur = 1,
			}
		end

		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if not head then
			return false
		end

		local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[tool.Name])
		if (tool._data.magCurrent.Value >= tool:GetAttribute("LastAmmo") or tool:GetAttribute("BulletHit") >= settings.amountPerRound) and settings.magCurrent ~= math.huge then
			return false
		end

		local rawDamage = math.random(settings.damage[1], settings.damage[2]) / settings.amountPerRound

		local dist = getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, target.Humanoid.RootPart)
		local minDamage = rawDamage / 2
		local damage = math.max(rawDamage - (dist / 20), minDamage)

		if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") ~= nil then
			tool:SetAttribute("BulletHit", tool:GetAttribute("BulletHit") + 1)
			local downed = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
			local headshot = isHead(hitPart, target)
			if settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, target, character}
				params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
				if not isObstacle(head, hitPart, params) then
					target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnHit", true)
					target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnCount", target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") + 1)
					if target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") <= 1 then
						task.spawn(function()
							while target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") >= 1 do
								task.wait(1.5)
								if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
									local n = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") - 1
									target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnCount", math.max(n, 0))
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					local lastBurnCount = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount")
					task.delay(1, function()
						if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and (target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") < lastBurnCount or target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") <= 0) then
							target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnHit", false)
						end
					end)
					target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(1.5)
					if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and target:FindFirstChild("Head") then
						local hurt = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.light_hurt), target.Head)
						hurt.Name = "hurt"
						hurt:Play()
					end
					if target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") >= 5 and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") < 10 and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") then
						task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 1, 0.1, {character = character, weapon = tool.Name}, true) end)
					elseif target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("BurnCount") == 15 then
						task.defer(function() _G.burn(target, 3, target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Burning") and 0 or 1, 0.1, {character = character, weapon = tool.Name}) end)
					end
				end
			else
				if headshot then
					damage *= settings.headshotMultiplier
				end
					--[[if downed then
						damage = math.max(damage / 3, 1)
					end]]
				if downed then
					damage *= 2
				end
				local armorAdjustedDamage, hasArmor, armorBroke = getArmorDamage(target, hitPart.Name, damage)
				if hasArmor then
					damage = armorAdjustedDamage

					if armorBroke and result then
						emitParticle("ArmorHitEffect", nil, result.Position + result.Normal)
					end
				end
				if humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
					damage /= 2
				end
				local wallPierced = getWallsPierced(head, hitPart)
				if wallPierced > 0 then
					damage = math.clamp(damage / (wallPierced + 1), damage / 3, damage)
				end

				if hitPart:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
					hitPart:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") - (damage * 1.5), 0))
					if headshot and hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
						damage = target.Humanoid.Health
					end
				end

				local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
				local dead = false
				local painAnim = ((target.Humanoid.Health - damage) / target.Humanoid.MaxHealth) * 100 < 15-- and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
				CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = damage, weapon = tool.Name})
				target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
				if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
					Storage.Events.hitPos:FireClient(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target), humanoid.RootPart, 3)
				end
				if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
					dead = true
				end
				if dead and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
					humanoid.Health += 20
				end
				if target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") or target.Humanoid.Health <= damage then
					local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
					force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
