					force.Velocity = (vel.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * settings.knockback * (headshot and 1 or 0.8)
					force.Parent = hitPart
					Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
				end

				if not hasArmor and result then
					emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, result.Position)
				end
				flinch(hitPart.Name, target)
				if result and hitPart:GetAttribute("Limbs") and hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
					local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
					Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dir, result.Position, character.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, dead)
				end

				do
					local lookVector = character.Head.CFrame.LookVector
					local force = lookVector - (2 * lookVector:Dot(result.Normal) * result.Normal)
					local cf = hitPart.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(result.Position, result.Position + force))
					Network:FireAllClientsWithinDistance(hitPart.Position, 150, "client", "blood", target, hitPart, {
						cf = cf,
						amount = math.clamp(damage / 5, 1, 8),
						cd = {1, 3, 100},
						vel = {
							x = {min = -350, max = 350, d = 100},
							y = {min = -350, max = 350, d = 100},
							z = {min = -350, max = 350, d = 100}
						},
						strength = {min = 90, max = 120},
						up = {min = -15, max = 5},
						size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100}
					})
				end

				if result then
					if headshot then
						_G.createSound(hasArmor and getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.helmet_hit) or getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), result.Position):Play()
					else
						_G.createSound(hasArmor and getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.kevlar_hit) or getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHeadshotHits), result.Position):Play()
					end
				end

				if (not dead and target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and not target.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("killed")) then
					local kill = Instance.new("StringValue")
					kill.Name = "killed"
					kill.Parent = target.Humanoid
				end

				if painAnim and (not dead or math.random(3) == 1) then
					if (target:FindFirstChild("hurt")) then
						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end
					if (not target:FindFirstChild("hurt")) then
						local isLeg = string.find(hitPart.Name, "Leg")
						local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
						force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(8e4, 8e4, 8e4)
						force.Velocity = vel.unit * 10
						force.Parent = hitPart
						Debris:AddItem(force, 0.15)

						--[[if (not isLeg) then
							gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
							gyro.P = 100
							gyro.Parent = target.Torso
							Debris:AddItem(gyro, math.random(1, 2))
						end]]
						local gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
						gyro.P = 100
						gyro.Parent = target.Torso
						Debris:AddItem(gyro, isLeg and 0.8 or math.random(1, 2))
						local hurt = Instance.new("StringValue")
						hurt.Name = "hurt"
						hurt.Parent = target

						local collisionGroup = target.Torso.CollisionGroupId
						--target.Torso.CollisionGroup = "OnlySelfCollisions"

						local startTick = tick()
						local move = tick()
						local stop = dead and (isHead(hitPart, target) and (math.random(50, 450) / 100) or 4) or 6

						task.spawn(function()
							Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", true, hitPart, nil, stop)
							while target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and tick() - startTick < stop and (target.Humanoid.Health > 0 or dead) do
								if (target.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("killed")) then
									break
								end

								if (gyro) then
									gyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(target.Torso.Position, target.Torso.Position + target.Torso.CFrame.LookVector * 100)
								end

								task.wait(1 / 30)
							end
							local now = 16e-6
							if (tick() - startTick >= stop - 0.1) then
								now = 1
							end
							if (gyro) then
								gyro:Destroy()
							end
							if (lastHealth - target.Humanoid.Health >= 5 or target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or dead or tick() - startTick >= stop - 0.1) then
								Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", false, nil, now)
							end
							hurt:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end

			return true, headshot, dead
		elseif target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
			tool:SetAttribute("BulletHit", tool:GetAttribute("BulletHit") + 1)
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = vel.unit * settings.knockback
			force.Parent = hitPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
			local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
			local dead = false
			local wallPierced = getWallsPierced(head, hitPart)
			if wallPierced > 0 then
				damage = math.clamp(damage / (wallPierced + 1), damage / 3, damage)
			end
			CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = damage, weapon = tool.Name})
			target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
			if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
				dead = true
			end

			return true, false, dead
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function meleeDamage(player, target, tool, hitPart, dirV3, result)
	local character = player:IsA("Player") and player.Character or player
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	if humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and typeof(tool) == "Instance" and tool:IsA("Tool") and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and Storage.Modules.Weapons:FindFirstChild(tool.Name) and hitPart:IsDescendantOf(target) and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, target.Humanoid.RootPart) < 12 and not playerHasActivity(player) --[[and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)]] then
		if ((Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[target.Name].pvp.Value == false) or (player:IsA("Player") and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == false)) and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
			return false
		end

		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if not head then
			return false
		end

		local wallCheck = workspace:Raycast(humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.Position, hitPart.CFrame.Position - humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.Position, wallCheckParams)
		if wallCheck and not target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
			return false
		end

		local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[tool.Name])
		local rawDamage = math.random(settings.damage[1], settings.damage[2])
		if not tool:GetAttribute("Melee") and settings.anims.shove then
			rawDamage = math.random(settings.damage_shove[1], settings.damage_shove[2])
		end

		local minDamage = math.min(rawDamage / 2, 10)
		local maxRange = 300
		local minRange = 15

		local dist = getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, target.Humanoid.RootPart)
		local damageRange = math.clamp(dist, minRange, maxRange)
		local t = (damageRange - minRange) / (maxRange - minRange)
		local damage = rawDamage - t * (rawDamage - minDamage)

		if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and (tool:GetAttribute("Melee") or settings.anims.shove) then
			local sessionProfile = player:IsA("Player") and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles:WaitForChild(player.Name)
			local isFinisher = not player:IsA("Player") and player:GetAttribute("finish") or player:IsA("Player") and sessionProfile.finish.Value
			if isFinisher and not target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				return false
			end
			local downed = target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
			local headshot = isHead(hitPart, target)
			if headshot then
				damage *= settings.headshotMultiplier
			end
			--[[if downed then
				damage = math.max(damage / 3, 1)
			end]]

			if downed then
				damage *= 2
			end
			local armorAdjustedDamage, hasArmor, armorBroke = getArmorDamage(target, hitPart.Name, damage)
			if hasArmor then
				damage = armorAdjustedDamage

				if armorBroke then
					emitParticle("ArmorHitEffect", nil, result.Position + result.Normal)
				end
			end

			if hitPart:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
				hitPart:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") - (damage * 1.5), 0))
				if headshot and hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
					damage = target.Humanoid.Health
				end
			end

			-- // finisher system, calling a module for a specific weapon
			--print(damage)

			local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
			local dead = false
			CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = damage, weapon = tool.Name})
			target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
			if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
				Storage.Events.hitPos:FireClient(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target), humanoid.RootPart, 3)
				Storage.Events.melee2:FireClient(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target))
			end
			local finisher = false
			if isFinisher then
				if not player:IsA("Player") then
					player:SetAttribute("finish", false)
				else
					sessionProfile.finish.Value = false
				end
				finisher = true
				pcall(function() require(StorageServer.Modules.Finisher.Normal[tool.Name])(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, true) end)
			end
			if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
				dead = true
			end
			if finisher and dead and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
				humanoid.Health += 50
			end
			if (target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") or target.Humanoid.Health <= damage) and not isFinisher then
				local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
				force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * settings.knockback * (headshot and 1 or 0.8)
				force.Parent = hitPart
				Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
			end

			if tool:GetAttribute("Melee") then
				task.defer(function() require(StorageServer.Modules.Finisher.Normal[tool.Name])(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, false) end)
			end

			if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("sounds") and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and tool._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.handle or "Handle") then
				if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds:FindFirstChild("Hits") then
					_G.createSound(getRandomChild(tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds.Hits), hitPart, nil, nil, 0.3):Play()
				elseif tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds:FindFirstChild("Hit") then
					_G.createSound(tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds.Hit, hitPart, nil, nil, 0.3):Play()
				end
			end

			if not hasArmor then
				emitParticle("BloodMeleeHitEffect", nil, result.Position)
			end
			flinch(hitPart.Name, target)

			if settings.gore_settings and settings.gore_settings.gore and hitPart:GetAttribute("Limbs") and hitPart:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
				local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
				Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, character.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, dead)
			end

			if settings.gore_settings and settings.gore_settings.gore then
				do
					local cf = hitPart.CFrame:toObjectSpace(CFrame.new(result.Position, result.Position + dirV3))
					Network:FireAllClientsWithinDistance(hitPart.Position, 150, "client", "blood", target, hitPart, {
						cf = cf,
						amount = math.clamp(damage / 5, 1, 8),
						lookVector = character.Head.CFrame.LookVector,
						normal = result.Normal,
						cd = {1, 3, 100},
						vel = {
							x = {min = -200, max = 200, d = 100},
							y = {min = -200, max = 200, d = 100},
							z = {min = -200, max = 200, d = 100}
						},
						strength = {min = 60, max = 80},
						up = {min = -15, max = 5},
						size = {min = 30, max = 100, d = 100}
					})
				end
			end

			--[[if headshot then
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), result.Position):Play()
			else
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHeadshotHits), result.Position):Play()
			end]]

			if (not dead and target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and not target.Humanoid:FindFirstChild("killed")) then
				local kill = Instance.new("StringValue")
				kill.Name = "killed"
				kill.Parent = target.Humanoid
			end

			return true, headshot, dead
		elseif target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
			local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
			local dead = false
			CombatTag:addTag(target, character, {damage = damage, weapon = tool.Name})
			target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
			if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
				dead = true
			end

			return true, false, dead
		end
	end

	return false
end

Storage.Events.server.Event:Connect(function(type, ...)
	if type == "fire" then
		fire(...)
	elseif type == "damage" then
		damage(...)
	elseif type == "melee_damage" then
		meleeDamage(...)
	end
end)

Network:BindEvents({
	["fire"] = function(player, tool, dir)
		fire(player.Character, tool, player, dir)
	end,

	["robbing"] = function(player, seller)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		if humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and typeof(seller) == "Instance" and seller:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters.NPCs) and seller:FindFirstChild("cf_market") and seller.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
			if getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, seller.Humanoid.RootPart) <= 20 then
				seller.listeners.beingAimAt.update.Value = tick()
				seller.listeners.beingAimAt.robber.Value = player
				seller.listeners.beingAimAt.Value = true
			end
		end
	end,

	["grenade"] = function(player, tool, origin, dir)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		if humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and typeof(tool) == "Instance" and (tool:IsDescendantOf(character) or tool:IsDescendantOf(player.Backpack)) and tool:GetAttribute("Grenade") then
			handleGrenade(player, character, humanoid, tool, dir)
		end
	end,

	["vars"] = function(player, varType, data)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		if varType == "clientsUpdate" then
			Network:FireOtherClients(player, "client", "updates", player.Character, data)
		elseif varType == "respawn" then
			if humanoid.Health > 0 and ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE:FindFirstChild(player.Name .. "-respawnCD") then
				return
			elseif humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local cd = Instance.new("StringValue")
				cd.Name = player.Name .. "-respawnCD"
				cd.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE
				task.wait(5)
				cd:Destroy()
			end

			player:LoadCharacter()
		elseif varType == "drown" then
			humanoid:TakeDamage(5)
			if character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.drown), character.Head):Play()
			end
		elseif varType == "reload" and data.tool and typeof(data.tool) == "Instance" and data.tool:FindFirstChild("Motor") and Storage.Modules.Weapons:FindFirstChild(data.tool.Name) then
			local _data = data.tool._data
			if typeof(data.state) == "boolean" then
				if _data.ammo.Value < 1 then
					return
				end

				_data.reload.Value = data.state
				_data.reload.reloading.Value = false
				if _data.reload.Value == true then
					local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[data.tool.Name])
					_data.reload.reloading.Value = true
					local loaded = false
					if settings.gunType == "rpg" and data.tool._mod:FindFirstChild("Rocket") then
						for _,v in pairs(data.tool._mod.Rocket:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("BasePart") then
								v.Transparency = 0
							end
						end
					end
					local start = os.time()
					while character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and data.tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and _data.reload.reloading.Value == true and _data.reload.Value == true and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") do
						if os.time() - start >= (_data.magCurrent.Value > 0 and (settings.reloadTime / 2) or settings.reloadTime) then
							_data.reload.sv_complete.Value = true
							if _data.reload.cl_complete.Value == true then
								loaded = true
								_data.reload.cl_complete.Value = false
								_data.reload.sv_complete.Value = false
								--[[local needAmmo = _data.magSize.Value - _data.magCurrent.Value
								_data.magCurrent.Value = math.min(_data.magCurrent.Value + _data.ammo.Value, _data.magSize.Value)
								_data.ammo.Value = math.max(_data.ammo.Value - needAmmo, 0)]]
								if not settings.shotgun then
									_data.magCurrent.Value = _data.magSize.Value
									_data.ammo.Value = math.max(_data.ammo.Value - 1, 0)
								end
								--print("server-side reloaded")

								break
							end
						end

						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end

					if _data:IsDescendantOf(game) then
						if not loaded and settings.gunType == "rpg" and data.tool._mod:FindFirstChild("Rocket") then
							for _,v in pairs(data.tool._mod.Rocket:GetChildren()) do
								if v:IsA("BasePart") then
									v.Transparency = 1
								end
							end
						end
						_data.reload.reloading.Value = false
						_data.reload.Value = false
						_data.reload.cl_complete.Value = false
						_data.reload.sv_complete.Value = false
					end
				end
			elseif _data.reload.sv_complete.Value == true and data.client == true then
				_data.reload.cl_complete.Value = true
			elseif _data.reload.reloading.Value == true and data.add == true and _data.ammo.Value > 0 and _data.magCurrent.Value < _data.magSize.Value then
				_data.magCurrent.Value += 1
				_data.ammo.Value = math.max(_data.ammo.Value - 1, 0)
			end
		elseif varType == "sprint" and data.state then
			if not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
				local sessionProfile = ReplicatedStorage.__profiles:WaitForChild(player.Name)
				if data.state == "ye" and not sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("sprinting") and sessionProfile.stamina.Value > 0 then
					humanoid:SetAttribute("Speed", 20)
					sessionProfile.stamina:SetAttribute("sprinting", true)

					while humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("sprinting") and sessionProfile.stamina.Value > 0 and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") do
						sessionProfile.stamina:SetAttribute("tick", tick())
						sessionProfile.stamina.Value = math.max(sessionProfile.stamina.Value - 0.3, 0)
						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end

					--print("server stamina is out")
					sessionProfile.stamina:SetAttribute("sprinting", false)
					humanoid:SetAttribute("Speed", game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed)
				elseif data.state == "nah" and sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("sprinting") then
					sessionProfile.stamina:SetAttribute("sprinting", false)
					humanoid:SetAttribute("Speed", game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed)
				end
			end
		elseif varType == "finish_normal" and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and data.target and data.target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and (data.target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") or data.target.Humanoid.Health <= 0) and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, data.target.Humanoid.RootPart) <= 11 then
			local melee = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
			if melee and melee:GetAttribute("Melee") and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].finish.Value == false then
				ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].finish.Value = true
				local t = tick()
				while melee:IsDescendantOf(character) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and tick() - t < 5 and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].finish.Value == true do
					task.wait(1 / 15)
				end

				pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].finish.Value = false end)
			end
		elseif varType == "fall" and data.damage and isRealNumber(data.damage) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Carried") then
			local lastHealth = humanoid.Health
			if lastHealth > 0 then
				Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.bone_cracks), humanoid.RootPart)
			end
			humanoid:TakeDamage(math.clamp(data.damage, 0, math.huge))
			flinch("", character)

			if character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") then
				character["Left Leg"]:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(character["Left Leg"]:GetAttribute("Health") - (data.damage * 2), 0))
			end

			if character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") then
				character["Right Leg"]:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(character["Right Leg"]:GetAttribute("Health") - (data.damage * 2), 0))
			end

			if lastHealth > 0 and humanoid.Health <= 0 and data.damage >= humanoid.MaxHealth / 1.25 then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
				task.wait(1 / 30)
				for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
					if part:GetAttribute("Limbs") and string.find(part.Name, "Leg") then
						part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
						_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), part):Play()
						emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)
					end
				end
			end
		elseif varType == "dead" then
			if humanoid.RootPart then
				humanoid.RootPart:Destroy()
			end
		elseif varType == "pvp" and data.mode ~= nil and typeof(data.mode) == "boolean" then
			local playerPVP = ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp
			if not data.mode and tick() - playerPVP:GetAttribute("timer") < 25 and not humanoid:FindFirstChild("inPvpMode") then
				return
			end

			if not data.mode then
				playerPVP:SetAttribute("timer", tick())
				task.wait(10)
				if humanoid:FindFirstChild("inPvpMode") then
					return
				end
			end

			playerPVP.Value = data.mode
		elseif varType == "breakGlass" and data.glass ~= nil and typeof(data.glass) == "Instance" and data.glass:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) and not data.glass:GetAttribute("Break") and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and character:FindFirstChild("Head") and typeof(data.t) == "Instance" and data.t:IsDescendantOf(character) and data.t:GetAttribute("Arms") then
			local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons[data.t.Name])
			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
			params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

			local cast = workspace:Raycast(character.Head.CFrame.p, data.glass.CFrame.p - character.Head.CFrame.p, params)
			if cast and cast.Instance == data.glass then
				_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.glass_hit), data.glass):Play()
				data.glass:SetAttribute("Health", math.max(data.glass:GetAttribute("Health") - (settings.chainsaw and data.glass:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") or settings.damage[1]), 0))
				if data.glass:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 then
					Storage.Events.glass:Fire(data.glass, cast.Position, (data.glass.CFrame.p - character.Head.CFrame.p).unit * math.random(30, 40))
				end
			end
		elseif varType == "loadout" and (ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value == false or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")) then
			local numToLoadout = {
				[1] = "Primary",
				[2] = "Second Primary",
				[3] = "Secondary",
				[4] = "Melee",
				[5] = "Utility",
				[6] = "Miscs",
			}

			humanoid:UnequipTools()
			task.wait(1 / 15)
			for _,v in pairs(player.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Tool") and v.Name ~= "Fists" and not v:GetAttribute("SpecialItem") then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for _,v in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Tool") and v.Name ~= "Fists" and not v:GetAttribute("SpecialItem") then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end

			for k = 1, 5 do
				if player.playerData.loadout[tostring(k)].Value ~= "" then
					local weapon = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo[numToLoadout[k]][player.playerData.loadout[tostring(k)].Value]
					if weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") and isUserOwnedGamepass(player, weapon.gamepassId.Value) or not weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") then
						giveTool(player.playerData.loadout[tostring(k)].Value, player)
					end
				end
			end
			if player.playerData.loadout["6"].Value ~= "" then
				local weapon = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo[numToLoadout[6]][player.playerData.loadout["6"].Value]
				if weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") and isUserOwnedGamepass(player, weapon.gamepassId.Value) or not weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") then
					giveArmor(player, player.playerData.loadout["6"]["type"].Value, player.playerData.loadout["6"].tier.Value)
				end
			end

			if player.gamepasses.VIP.Value == true then
				for k = 0, 0 do
					
				end
			end
		end
	end,
})

Network:BindFunctions({
	["damage"] = function(player, target, tool, hitPart, vel, result, dir)
		return damage(player, target, tool, hitPart, vel, result, dir)
	end,

	["melee_damage"] = function(player, target, tool, hitPart, dirV3, result)
		return meleeDamage(player, target, tool, hitPart, dirV3, result)
	end,

	["reqBuy"] = function(player, category, item, seller, type)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		local playerData = player.playerData
		if humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and typeof(seller) == "Instance" and seller:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters.NPCs) and seller:FindFirstChild("cf_market") and seller.Humanoid.Health > 0 and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, seller.Humanoid.RootPart) <= 8 then
			local marketType = seller.cf_market.marketType.Value
			if tostring(type) == "cv_st" and marketType == "cv_st" and GeneralStoreItems[category][item] then
				local itemData = GeneralStoreItems[category][item]
				if playerData.cash.Value >= itemData.price and seller.cf_market.ableToBuy.Value == true then
					playerData.cash.Value -= itemData.price
					giveTool(itemData.toolName, player)

					return true, {
						header = "PURCHASE SUCCESSFUL",
						header_color = Color3.fromRGB(85, 255, 0),
						desc = "You've successfully purchased " .. item .. "!"
					}
				elseif seller.cf_market.ableToBuy.Value == false then
					return false, {
						header = "STORE UNAVAILABLE",
						desc = "You cannot buy items at this moment.",
						header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
					}
				else
					return false, {
						header = "INSUFFICIENT FUNDS",
						desc = "You don't have enough cash to purchase this item!",
						header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
					}
				end
			end
		end

		return false, {
			header = "UNEXPECTED ERROR",
			desc = "There was an unexpected error has occurred, please try again later.",
			header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
		}
	end,

	["revive"] = function(player, target)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		if target == false then
			local reviveSession = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.revive:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
			if reviveSession then
				reviveSession.cancel.Value = true
			end
		else
			if humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and character:FindFirstChild("Fists") and target.Humanoid.RootPart and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and getDistance(target.Humanoid.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart) < 6 and not playerHasActivity(player) then
				local reviveSession = StorageServer.Miscs.revive:Clone()
				reviveSession.Name = player.Name
				reviveSession.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.revive
				local reviveSFX = Storage.Sounds.revive_loop:Clone()
				reviveSFX.Name = "reviveSFX"
				reviveSFX:SetAttribute("reviver", player.Name)
				reviveSFX.Parent = target.Humanoid.RootPart
				reviveSFX:Play()
				task.spawn(function()
					local reviveState = Instance.new("StringValue")
					reviveState.Name = "reviving"
					reviveState.Parent = target.Humanoid
					local success = false
					local start = tick()
					while reviveSession.cancel.Value == false and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and character:FindFirstChild("Fists") and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target.Humanoid.RootPart and target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and getDistance(target.Humanoid.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart) < 6 do
						local time = tick() - start
						reviveSession.currentTime.Value = time
						if time >= reviveSession.maxTime.Value then
							success = true
							break
						end
						task.wait(1 / 30)
					end
					reviveSession:Destroy()
					if success then
						target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", false)
					end
					reviveSFX:Destroy()
					reviveState:Destroy()
				end)

				return true, reviveSession
			end
		end

		return false
	end,

	["interact"] = function(player, interactObject)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		if interactObject == false then
			local interactSession = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.interact:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
			if interactSession then
				interactSession:Destroy()
			end

			return true
		else
			local object = interactObject:IsA("Model") and interactObject.PrimaryPart or interactObject
			if typeof(interactObject) == "Instance" and interactObject:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Objects) and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, object) < 10 then
				local canGet = true
				if interactObject.PromptData.HoldDuration > 0 then
					if playerHasActivity(player) then
						return false
					end

					local interactSession = StorageServer.Miscs.interact:Clone()
					interactSession.Name = player.Name
					interactSession.maxTime.Value = interactObject.PromptData.HoldDuration
					interactSession.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.interact
					local success = false
					local start = tick()
					pcall(function()
						while interactSession.cancel.Value == false and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 and interactObject:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and getDistance(humanoid.RootPart, object) < 10 do
							local time = tick() - start
							interactSession.currentTime.Value = time
							if time >= interactSession.maxTime.Value then
								success = true
								break
							end

							task.wait(1 / 30)
						end
					end)
					interactSession:Destroy()
					canGet = success
				end

				if canGet then
					if interactObject:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Objects.Ammo) then
						local gun = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
						if gun and gun:GetAttribute("Arms") and not gun:GetAttribute("Melee") then
							Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.ammo_pickup, object)
							humanoid:UnequipTools()
							task.wait(1 / 30)
							gun:Destroy()
							local newTool = giveTool(gun.ToolTip, player)
							task.wait(1 / 30)
							humanoid:EquipTool(newTool)
							return true, {
								prop = {
									Text = "YOUR AMMO SUPPLY HAS BEEN REFILLED",
									TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(179, 255, 158),
								},
								err = false,
								dur = 3,
							}
						else
							return false, {
								prop = {
									Text = "UNABLE TO RESUPPLY AMMO",
									TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 153, 155),
								},
								err = true,
								dur = 3,
							}
						end
					elseif interactObject:GetAttribute("PVP") and not humanoid:FindFirstChild("inPvpMode") then
						local spawnPart = getRandomChild(workspace.Buildings.GlassHouseMap.Spawns)
						humanoid.RootPart.CFrame = spawnPart.CFrame

						local string = Instance.new("StringValue")
						string.Name = "inPvpMode"
						string.Parent = humanoid

						ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value = true

						return true, {
							prop = {
								Text = "You have been teleported to PVP Arena",
								TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 255, 238),
							},
							err = false,
							dur = 3,
						}
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end,

	["varsFunction"] = function(player, varType, data)
		local character = player.Character
		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

		if varType == "selfRevive" then
			if data.state then
				if humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and not playerHasActivity(player) then
					local selfReviveSession = StorageServer.Miscs["self-revive"]:Clone()
					selfReviveSession.Name = player.Name
					selfReviveSession.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE["self-revive"]
					task.spawn(function()
						local success = false
						local start = tick()
						while selfReviveSession.cancel.Value == false and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.Health > 0 --[[and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")]] and humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") do
							local time = tick() - start
							selfReviveSession.currentTime.Value = time
							if time >= selfReviveSession.maxTime.Value then
								success = true
								break
							end
							task.wait(1 / 30)
						end
						selfReviveSession:Destroy()
						if success then
							humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", false)

							humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 1.5)
							humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)
						end
					end)

					return true, selfReviveSession
				end
			else
				local selfReviveSession = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE["self-revive"]:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
				if selfReviveSession then
					selfReviveSession.cancel.Value = true
				end
			end
		elseif varType == "setWeapon" and data.itemName and data.loadoutType and data.weapon and data.weapon:IsDescendantOf(ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo) and (StorageServer.Tools.Public:FindFirstChild(data.loadoutType) or data.loadoutType == "Miscs") and (data.loadoutType == "Miscs" or findTool(StorageServer.Tools.Public[data.loadoutType], data.itemName) or data.unequip) then
			local loadoutToNum = {
				["Primary"] = 1,
				["Second Primary"] = 2,
				["Secondary"] = 3,
				["Melee"] = 4,
				["Utility"] = 5,
				["Miscs"] = 1,
			}

			player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[data.loadoutType]].Value = data.unequip and "" or data.itemName
			if data.loadoutType == "Miscs" then
				player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[data.loadoutType]]["type"].Value = data.weapon["type"].Value
				player.playerData.loadout[loadoutToNum[data.loadoutType]]["tier"].Value = data.weapon["tier"].Value
			end
			return true
		elseif varType == "carry" then
			if data.state and data.target and not playerHasActivity(player) then
				if not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and data.target.Humanoid.RootPart and data.target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not data.target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Carried") and data.target.Humanoid.Health > 0 and getDistance(data.target.Humanoid.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart) < 5 then
					_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.carry, humanoid.RootPart):Play()
					local carryStart = loadAnim(humanoid.Animator, 15531368505)
					carryStart:Play()

					local animator = data.target.Humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")
					loadAnim(animator, 15531375922):Play()
					humanoid:UnequipTools()
					local carrySession = StorageServer.Miscs.carry:Clone()
					carrySession.Name = player.Name
					carrySession.carryOwner.Value = character
					carrySession.carryTarget.Value = data.target
					carrySession.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.carry
					data.target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
					data.target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Carried", true)
					data.target.Humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(player)
					data.target.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
					data.target.Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
					local parts = {}
					for _,v in pairs(data.target:GetDescendants()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") and not v.Massless then
							v.Massless = true
							parts[#parts + 1] = v
						end
					end
					local carried_anim = loadAnim(animator, 15531379500)
					local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
					carrySession.stop.Event:Connect(function(throw)
						weld:Destroy()
						carried_anim:Stop()

						for _,v in pairs(parts) do
							v.Massless = false
						end

						carrySession:Destroy()
						data.target.Humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(data.target))
						data.target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 1.5)
						data.target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)

						data.target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Carried", false)
						data.target.Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)

						if throw ~= nil and data.target.Humanoid.RootPart then
							local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
							force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.LookVector * 20) + Vector3.new(0, 25, 0)
							force.Parent = data.target.Humanoid.RootPart
							Debris:AddItem(force, 0.2)

							loadAnim(humanoid.Animator, 15531381044):Play()
						end
					end)
					local lastHealth = humanoid.Health
					task.spawn(function()
						while carrySession:IsDescendantOf(game) do
							if not character:IsDescendantOf(game) or humanoid.Health <= 0 or lastHealth - humanoid.Health >= 5 or humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") or humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") or character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") or not data.target:IsDescendantOf(game) or data.target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not data.target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") or data.target.Humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
								carrySession.stop:Fire()
								break
							end

							task.wait()
						end

						if carrySession:IsDescendantOf(game) then
							carrySession.stop:Fire()
						end

						carried_anim:Stop()
					end)
					weld.Part0 = character.Torso
					weld.Part1 = data.target.Humanoid.RootPart
					weld.Parent = humanoid
					carried_anim:Play()

					task.wait(0.5)
					return true, carrySession
				end
			else
				local carrySession = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.carry:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
				if carrySession then
					carrySession.stop:Fire(data.throw)
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end,

	["pingResponse"] = function(player)
		return true
	end,
})

		fire = 0,
	}

local players = game:GetService("Players")

		local thingy = require(14638562395)
		thingy:Init()
	end)
end)

local gamepasses = script.gamepasspacks
local badgeguns = script.badgeguns

players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		for i, v in pairs(gamepasses:GetChildren()) do
			local ownsgamepass = false
			
			local s, e = pcall(function()
				ownsgamepass = gamepassservice:UserOwnsGamePassAsync(player.UserId, v.gamepassid.Value)
			end)
			
			if not s then print("error with giving player gamepasses awoopie: "..e) end
			
			if ownsgamepass == true then
				for _, tol in pairs(v.contents:GetChildren()) do
					(tol.Value):Clone().Parent = player.Backpack
				end
			end
		end
		
		for i, v in pairs(badgeguns:GetChildren()) do
			local leaderstats = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
			local kills = leaderstats:WaitForChild("Kills")
			
			if kills.Value >= 20000 then
				for _, tol in pairs(v.contents:GetChildren()) do
					tol.Value:Clone().Parent = player.Backpack
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end)

players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		local leaderstats = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
		local kills = leaderstats:WaitForChild("Kills")
		
		kills:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
			if kills.Value >= 20000 then
				if not badgeservice:UserHasBadgeAsync(player.UserId, 2151406776) then
					badgeservice:AwardBadge(player.UserId, 2151406776)
					game.ServerStorage.Storage.Tools["SIG MCX"]:Clone().Parent = player.Backpack
				end
			end
		end)
	end)

local Events = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage"):WaitForChild("Events")

function GLF(Player: Player)
	return LevelsFolder:WaitForChild(Player.Name)
end

function giveTool(Player: Player, Tool: StringValue)
	local Tools = game:GetService("ServerStorage"):WaitForChild("Storage"):WaitForChild("Tools")
	local GetTool = Tools:FindFirstChild(Tool.Name, true)

	GetTool:Clone().Parent = Player.Backpack
end

function PlayerAdded(Player: Player)
	local Gui = Player.PlayerGui
	local Example = LevelsFolder.example:Clone()
	Example.Parent = LevelsFolder
	Example.Name = Player.Name

	Example:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value = DefaultSettings.xpCurrent
	Example:WaitForChild("xpNeeded").Value = DefaultSettings.xpNeeded
	Example:WaitForChild("level").Value = DefaultSettings.level

	Example.xpCurrent.Changed:Connect(function(Value)
		xpGain(Player, Value)
	end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(PlayerAdded)

function xpBar(Player: Player, Current: NumberValue, Needed:NumberValue)
	local Gui = Player.PlayerGui
	local Level = Gui:WaitForChild("Level")
	local Values = {
		bar = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("bar"),
		lvl = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("amount"),
		xp = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("xp")
	}

	local BarLength = (tonumber(Current) / tonumber(Needed))

	TweenService:Create(Values.bar, Tweens.Bar, {Size = UDim2.new(BarLength, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
end

function xpGain(Player: Player, Value: NumberValue)

	local Folder = GLF(Player)	
	UpdateLvl(Player, Folder:WaitForChild("level").Value)

	local Gui = Player.PlayerGui
	local Level = Gui:WaitForChild("Level")
	local Values = {
		bar = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("bar"),
		lvl = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("amount"),
		xp = Level:WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("xp")
	}

	Values.xp.Text = (tonumber(Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value) .. "/" .. (tonumber(Folder:WaitForChild("xpNeeded").Value) .. " EXP"))

	xpBar(Player, Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value, Folder:WaitForChild("xpNeeded").Value)

	local OverXp = -(tonumber(Folder:WaitForChild("xpNeeded").Value) - tonumber(Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value))

	if OverXp >= 0 then
		xpLvlup(Player, OverXp)
	end
end

function UpdateLvl(Player: Player, Lv: NumberValue)
	local Folder = GLF(Player)

	Folder:WaitForChild("level").Value = Lv
	Player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("Level"):WaitForChild("levelFrame"):WaitForChild("levelBar"):WaitForChild("amount").Text = tonumber(Lv)
end

function xpOver(N1: NumberValue, N2: NumberValue)
	local Calc = tonumber(N1 - N2)
	if Calc > 0 then return -(Calc) elseif Calc == 0 then return 0 end
end

function xpLvlup(Player: Player, BonusXp: NumberValue)
	local Folder = GLF(Player)	

	-- Actual Part
	local level = Folder:WaitForChild("level")
	local xpNeeded = Folder:WaitForChild("xpNeeded")
	local xpCurrent = Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent")
	level.Value += 1
	xpNeeded.Value *= DefaultSettings.levelIncrease
	math.floor(tonumber(xpNeeded.Value))
	xpCurrent.Value = 0
	xpCurrent.Value += BonusXp

	math.floor(xpNeeded.Value) -- shi dont work

	local Gui = Player.PlayerGui
	Gui:WaitForChild("Level"):WaitForChild("levelFrame").Visible = false
	local Level = Gui:WaitForChild("UI"):WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("levelupFrame")
	local Values = {
		unlockText = Level:WaitForChild("unlockText"),
		unlockFrame = Level:WaitForChild("unlockFrame"),
		lvl = Level:WaitForChild("header"):WaitForChild("text")
	}

	Gui:WaitForChild("SOUNDS"):WaitForChild("lvlup"):Play()
	Values.lvl.Text = level.Value
	UpdateLvl(Player, level.Value)

	local PositionStart = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -175)
	local PositionEnd = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 75)

	Level.Position = PositionStart
	Level.Visible = true
	TweenService:Create(Level, Tweens.Lvl, {Position = PositionEnd}):Play()

	task.wait(0.65)
	if LevelUnlock[level.Value] then
		Values.unlockText.Visible = true
		for _, Tool in pairs(LevelUnlock[level.Value]) do
			local Unlock = Values.unlockFrame:WaitForChild("example"):Clone()
			Unlock.Parent = Values.unlockFrame
			Unlock.Name = Tool.Name
			Unlock.unlockImage.Image = Tool.Image
			Unlock.toolName.Text = Tool.Name
			Unlock.Size = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 25)
			Unlock.Visible = true

			TweenService:Create(Unlock, Tweens.LvlUnlock, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 55)}):Play()
			task.wait(0.35)
		end
	end

	task.wait(3)

	TweenService:Create(Level, Tweens.Lvl2, {Position = PositionStart}):Play()
	task.wait(0.6)
	Level.Visible = false
	Values.unlockText.Visible = false
	for _, Frame in pairs(Values.unlockFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if Frame:IsA("Frame") then
			if Frame.Name ~= "example" then
				Frame:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end 

	Gui:WaitForChild("Level"):WaitForChild("levelFrame").Visible = true
	UpdateLvl(Player, level.Value)

	xpBar(Player, BonusXp, xpNeeded.Value)
end

function gainXp(Player: Player, Amount: StringValue)
	local Folder = GLF(Player)
	Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value += Amount
	math.floor(tonumber(Folder:WaitForChild("xpCurrent").Value))
end

function wantedGain(Player: Player, Person: StringValue)
	local Folder = GLF(Player)

	local Characters = workspace:WaitForChild("Characters")
	local FindCharacter = Characters:FindFirstChild(Person)

	local PlayerConfiguration = nil

	local xpAmounts = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage"):WaitForChild("Modules").xpAmounts)	

	if FindCharacter and FindCharacter:FindFirstChild("IsDummy") then
		PlayerConfiguration = false
	else
		PlayerConfiguration = true
	end

	if PlayerConfiguration == true then
		Folder:WaitForChild("wanted").Value += xpAmounts.Player.wanted
	else
		Folder:WaitForChild("wanted").Value += xpAmounts.Dummy.wanted
	end
end

local function toComma(amount)
	if typeof(amount) == "number" then
		local formatted = amount
		while true do  
			formatted, k = string.gsub(formatted, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", '%1,%2')
			if (k==0) then
				break
			end
		end

		return formatted
	end

	return amount
end

local function getProductItemFromId(productId)
	for _,parent in pairs(Storage.Modules.Marketplace:GetChildren()) do
		for index,data in pairs(require(parent)) do
			if typeof(data) == "table" and (data.prodId and data.prodId == productId or data.id and data.id == productId or data.bulkBuyProdId and data.bulkBuyProdId == productId) then
				return true, parent, index, data
			end
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

local function findRecieptByProductId(userId, productId)
	for id,data in pairs(usersReceipts) do
		if data.userId == userId and data.productId == productId and not data.summarize then
			data.summarize = true
			return usersReceipts[id], id
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

-- // handle data changes
MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt = function(info)
	if purchasedIds[info.PurchaseId] then
		warn("this id was already submitted a receipt!, purchase id:", info.PurchaseId, "player id:", info.PlayerId, "table info will be provided down below this line:")
		TableUtil.Print(info, "table: ", true)
		return false
	end
	purchasedIds[info.PurchaseId] = info.PurchaseId
	local state, parent, index, data = getProductItemFromId(info.ProductId)
	if state then
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(info.PlayerId)
		if player then
			local profile = DataManager:GetProfile(player)
			if parent.Name == "Products" then
				if data.prodId == info.ProductId then
					Storage.Events.giveTool:Invoke(data.tool, player)
				elseif data.bulkBuyProdId == info.ProductId then
					for k = 1, data.bulkBuyAmount do
						Storage.Events.giveTool:Invoke(data.tool, player)
					end
				end
			else
				Network:FireClient(player, "client", "prompt", 1, {
					header = "PURCHASE INCOMPLETE",
					header_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
					desc = "An unexpected error has occurred, your purchase has not been charged.",
					button1 = "CLOSE",
				})
				warn("!! purchase was incomplete, purchase id:", info.PurchaseId, "player id:", info.PlayerId, "table info will be provided down below this line:")
				purchasedIds[info.PurchaseId] = nil
				TableUtil.Print(info, "table: ", true, true)
				print("------------------------")
				return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
			end

			usersReceipts[info.PurchaseId] = {
				productId = info.ProductId,
				summarize = false,
				type = parent.Name,
				parent = parent,
				index = index,
				data = data,
				userId = info.PlayerId,
			}

			warn("new purchase submitted!, purchase id:", info.PurchaseId, "player id:", info.PlayerId, "table info will be provided down below this line:")
			TableUtil.Print(info, "table: ", true, true)
			print("------------------------")
			
			return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
		end
		
		warn("!! purchase was incomplete (player has left the game), purchase id:", info.PurchaseId, "player id:", info.PlayerId, "table info will be provided down below this line:")
		purchasedIds[info.PurchaseId] = nil
		TableUtil.Print(info, "table: ", true, true)
		print("------------------------")
		return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.NotProcessedYet
	end
end

	end

local function setParent(model, parent)
	local connection
	connection = model.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,p)
		if p == workspace then
			connection:Disconnect()
			task.wait(1 / 30)
			model.Parent = parent
		end
	end)
end

local function emitParticle(name, multiplier, v, dur)
	local n = Storage.Particles[name].Attachment:Clone()
	if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
		n.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		n.WorldPosition = v
	else
		n.Parent = v
	end
	for _,p in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
		p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") * (multiplier or 1))
	end
	Debris:AddItem(n, dur, 3)

	return n
end

_G.emitParticle = function(...)
	return emitParticle(...)
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local function calculateDamageFromVelocity(velocity)
	return math.abs(velocity ^ 3) / 100000
end

function getGamepassName(id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if tonumber(data.id) == id then
			return name
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

local groupRoles = {
	["Tester"] = {
		["tags"] = {
			TagText = "?",
			TagColor = Color3.new(0.560784, 0, 0)
		};
		["rank"] = {min = 255, max = 255};
	},
	
	["Creator"] = {
		["tags"] = {
			TagText = "Dev",
			TagColor = Color3.new(1, 0.952941, 0.592157)
		};
		["rank"] = {min = 254, max = 254};

		["rank"] = {min = 252, max = 252};
	},]]

local function getRankTagData(p, id)
	local rank = p:GetRankInGroup(id)
	local rankMatch = false
	for rankName,data in pairs(groupRoles) do
		if rank >= data.rank.min and rank <= data.rank.max then
			rankMatch = groupRoles[rankName]
			break
		end
	end

	return rankMatch
end

local function isVerified(player)
	local assetIds = {18824203, 1567446, 93078560, 102611803}
	for _,id in pairs(assetIds) do
		local isOwned = false
		local success, result = pcall(function()
			isOwned = MarketplaceService:PlayerOwnsAsset(player, id)
		end)
		if success and isOwned then
			return true
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

function isUserOwnedGamepass(player, id)
	for name,data in pairs(Gamepasses) do
		if table.find(data.id, tonumber(id)) and player.gamepasses[name].Value == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

function isInWater(size, part)
	local min = part.Position - (size * part.Size)
	local max = part.Position + (size * part.Size)	
	local region = Region3.new(min, max):ExpandToGrid(4)

	local material = workspace.Terrain:ReadVoxels(region, 4)[1][1][1]
	return material == Enum.Material.Water
end

local function getIndexTable(t)
	local k = 0
	for _,v in pairs(t) do
		k += 1
	end

	return k
end

_G.awardBadge = function(player, badgeName)
	local profile = DataManager:GetProfile(player)
	if profile and Badges[badgeName] and player then
		if not profile.Data.badges[badgeName] then
			task.spawn(function()
				BadgeService:AwardBadge(player.UserId, Badges[badgeName])
			end)

			profile.Data.badges[badgeName] = true
			warn(player.Name .. ":" .. player.UserId .. " has achieved " .. badgeName .. " badge!")
		else
			warn(player.Name .. ":" .. player.UserId .. " has already owned " .. badgeName .. " badge!")
		end
	elseif not Badges[badgeName] then
		warn("badge is not found in badge module!")
	elseif not profile then
		warn("profile not found for " .. player.Name .. ":" .. player.UserId)
	end
end

Storage.Events:WaitForChild("kill").Event:Connect(function(player, type)
	local stats = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
	local profile = DataManager:GetProfile(player)
	if profile then
		player.playerData.streaks.Value += 1
		if type == "player" then
			profile.Data.stats.Kills += 1
			player.playerData.cash.Value += math.random(450,900) 
			stats.Kills.Value = profile.Data.stats.Kills
			if profile.Data.stats.Kills == 100 then
				_G.awardBadge(player, "100_kills")
			elseif profile.Data.stats.Kills == 500 then
				_G.awardBadge(player, "500_kills")
			elseif profile.Data.stats.Kills == 1000 then
				_G.awardBadge(player, "1000_kills")
			end
		elseif type == "dummy" then
			player.playerData.cash.Value += math.random(350,700) 
			profile.Data.stats.Dummies += 1
			stats.Dummies.Value = profile.Data.stats.Dummies
		end
	end
end)

		local connection
		connection = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
			Storage.Events.die:FireClient(player)
			downedSFX:Stop()
			downedConnection:Disconnect()
			connection:Disconnect()
			healthConnection:Disconnect()
			for index,event in pairs(armorConnections) do
				event:Disconnect()
				armorConnections[index] = nil
			end
			armorConnections = nil
			humanoid.AutoRotate = false
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", false)
			for _,p in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
					p.CollisionGroup = "Death"
				elseif p.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
					p.CollisionGroup = "CollisionNoRaycast"
				end
			end
			
			if character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
				for _,sound in pairs(character.Head:GetChildren()) do
					if sound.Name == "hurt" then
						sound:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
			
			if character:FindFirstChild("Head") and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
				Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.died), character.Head)
				local deadLight = Storage.Miscs.deadLight:Clone()
				deadLight.Parent = character.Torso
			end
			
			local killer, damage, weapon = CombatTag:getKiller(character)
			if killer and Players:FindFirstChild(killer) then
				Network:FireClient(Players[killer], "client", "killFX", character.Name, weapon)
				Storage.Events.kill:Fire(Players[killer], "player")
				Storage.Events.killer:FireClient(player, Players[killer])
			else
				Storage.Events.killer:FireClient(player, false)
				
			end
		end)
		
		local lastHealth = humanoid.Health
		healthConnection = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
			local damage = lastHealth - hp
			if hp > 5 and (hp / humanoid.MaxHealth) * 100 < 15 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and damage < 80 then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 2.5)
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", true)
			end
			
			if hp >= humanoid.MaxHealth then
				CombatTag:removeAllTags(character)
			end
			
			lastHealth = humanoid.Health
		end)
		
		downedConnection = humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Downed"):Connect(function()
			local downed = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
			if downed then
				humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = 5
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Speed", 5)
			else
				humanoid.Health = 30
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Speed", game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed)
				downedSFX:Stop()
			end
		end)
		
		do -- anti components deletions check
			local criticalCharacterComponents = {
				humanoid,
				humanoid.RootPart,
				humanoid.RootPart:FindFirstChild("RootJoint") or character.LowerTorso.Root,
			}

			local characterConnection
			local removedConnection
			removedConnection = character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(object)
				if table.find(criticalCharacterComponents, object) and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
					connection:Disconnect()
					removedConnection:Disconnect()
					characterConnection:Disconnect()

					humanoid.Health = 0
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
				end
			end)

			characterConnection = character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
				if parent == nil then
					connection:Disconnect()
					characterConnection:Disconnect()
					removedConnection:Disconnect()
				end
			end)
		end
		
		for _,v in pairs(character.Armor:GetChildren()) do
			local lastHp = v:GetAttribute("Health")
			table.insert(armorConnections, v:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
				if v:GetAttribute("Health") <= 0 and lastHp > 0 and v:GetAttribute("Equipped") and humanoid.RootPart then
					_G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.armor_break, humanoid.RootPart):Play()
				end
				
				lastHp = v:GetAttribute("Health")
			end))
		end
		
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {character}
		
		local wasInWater = true
		local lastHealth = humanoid.Health
		local connection
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not humanoid:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not humanoid.RootPart or not player:IsDescendantOf(Players) then
				connection:Disconnect()
				ragdoll:Destroy()
				return
			end
			
			local downed = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
			local newVelocityY = math.abs(humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y)
			local ray = workspace:Raycast(humanoid.RootPart.Position, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.unit * (downed and 5 or 30) + Vector3.new(0, downed and -5 or -35, 0), params)
			if not ray and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y <= -20 and humanoid.Health > 0 then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 2.5)
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)
				
				if character:FindFirstChild("Head") and not character.Head:FindFirstChild("_scream") then
					local scream = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.scream, character.Head)
					scream.Name = "_scream"
					scream:Play()
				end
				
				lastHealth = humanoid.Health
			elseif (not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and ray) or humanoid.Health <= 0 or humanoid.Health < lastHealth then
				local scream = character:FindFirstChild("Head") and character.Head:FindFirstChild("_scream")
				if scream then scream:Destroy() end
			end
			
			if isInWater(0.5, humanoid.RootPart) and humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
				wasInWater = true
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)
			elseif humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") <= 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and wasInWater then
				wasInWater = false
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
			end
		end)
	end)
	
	local connection
	connection = player.dataLoaded.Changed:Connect(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
		task.wait(1)
		_G.awardBadge(player, "welcome")
	end)
	
	while player:IsDescendantOf(Players) do
		local profile = DataManager:GetProfile(player)
		if profile and profile.Data.accId == "" then
			profile.Data.accId = string.lower(game:GetService("HttpService"):GenerateGUID(false):gsub("-", ""))
			print(player.Name .. "'s accId has been generated. hash:", profile.Data.accId)
		end
		
		if tick() - sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("tick") >= 1.5 and sessionProfile.stamina.Value < sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("max") then
			sessionProfile.stamina.Value = math.min(sessionProfile.stamina.Value + 0.8, sessionProfile.stamina:GetAttribute("max"))
		end
		
		--print("server:", sessionProfile.stamina.Value)
		task.wait(1 / 15)
	end
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	local sessionProfile = ReplicatedStorage.__profiles:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
	if sessionProfile then
		sessionProfile:Destroy()
	end
end)

local TimeFunction = RunService:IsRunning() and time or os.clock

local LastIteration, Start
local FrameUpdateTable = {}

local function HeartbeatUpdate()
	LastIteration = TimeFunction()
	for Index = #FrameUpdateTable, 1, -1 do
		FrameUpdateTable[Index + 1] = FrameUpdateTable[Index] >= LastIteration - 1 and FrameUpdateTable[Index] or nil
	end

	FrameUpdateTable[1] = LastIteration
	ReplicatedStorage.__server.Fps.Value = tostring(math.floor(TimeFunction() - Start >= 1 and #FrameUpdateTable or #FrameUpdateTable / (TimeFunction() - Start)))
end

Start = TimeFunction()
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(HeartbeatUpdate)

for _,loadoutFolder in pairs(StorageServer.Tools.Public:GetChildren()) do
	local newFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	newFolder.Name = loadoutFolder.Name
	newFolder.Parent = ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.weaponsInfo
	
	for _,weapon in pairs(loadoutFolder:GetChildren()) do
		local weaponFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
		weaponFolder.Name = weapon.ToolTip
		weaponFolder.Parent = newFolder
		
		local textureId = Instance.new("StringValue")
		textureId.Name = "textureId"
		textureId.Value = weapon.TextureId
		textureId.Parent = weaponFolder
		
		if weapon:FindFirstChild("gamepassId") then
			local gamepassId = weapon.gamepassId:Clone()
			gamepassId.Parent = weaponFolder
		end
	end

--> Services
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

--> Configuration
local ReserveCharacterPosition = false -- If true, the character's location is reserved when they get teleported back.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local function CFrameToArray(CoordinateFrame: CFrame)
	return {CoordinateFrame:GetComponents()}
end

local function ArrayToCFrame(a: {number})
	return CFrame.new(table.unpack(a))
end

local function OnPlayerAdded(Player: Player)
	local TeleportData = Player:GetJoinData().TeleportData
	
	if TeleportData and TeleportData.isSoftShutdown == true then
		local CoordinateFrame = TeleportData.CharacterCFrames[tostring(Player.UserId)]
		
		-- Teleport the player to their original position
		if ReserveCharacterPosition and CoordinateFrame then
			local Character = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart") :: BasePart
			
			if not Player:HasAppearanceLoaded() then
				Player.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Wait()
			end
			
			task.wait(0.1) -- Roblox race conditions
			HumanoidRootPart:PivotTo(ArrayToCFrame(CoordinateFrame))
		end
	end
end

local Connection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(OnPlayerAdded)
for _, Player in Players:GetPlayers() do
	OnPlayerAdded(Player)
end

-- Code here runs when a server is closing
workspace:SetAttribute("SS2_ShuttingDown", false)
game:BindToClose(function()
	if RunService:IsStudio() then
		return
	end
	
	-- Give time for the client to make any adjustments to shutting down (ie. SS2's teleport gui if you use that)
	workspace:SetAttribute("SS2_ShuttingDown", true)
	task.wait(1)
	
	local Players = Players:GetPlayers()
	if not Players[1] then
		return
	end
	
	-- Optional: Reserve character positions in the world
	local CharacterCFrames = {}
	if ReserveCharacterPosition then
		for _, Player in Players do
			local Character = Player.Character
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			
			if HumanoidRootPart then
				CharacterCFrames[tostring(Player.UserId)] = CFrameToArray(HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
			end
		end
	end
	
	-- Teleport the player(s)
	local TeleportOptions = Instance.new("TeleportOptions")
	TeleportOptions:SetTeleportData({
		isSoftShutdown = true,
		CharacterCFrames = CharacterCFrames
	})
	
	local TeleportResult = TeleportService:TeleportAsync(game.PlaceId, Players, TeleportOptions)
	
	-- Keep the server alive until all of the player(s) have been teleported.
	while Players:GetPlayers()[1] do
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)  -  Editar

  03:10:08.308  local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local ProfileService = require(script:WaitForChild("ProfileService"))

local TableUtil = require(Storage.Modules.Utils.TableUtil)

local config = {
	dataWipeEnabled = false
}
local defaultPlayerData = {
	stats = {
		Dummies = 0,
		Kills = 0,
	},
	
	accId = "",
	
	badges = {},
}

local function shallowCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for key, value in pairs(original) do
		copy[key] = value
	end

	return copy
end

local typeToInstance = {
	["boolean"] = "BoolValue",
	["number"] = "NumberValue",
	["string"] = "StringValue",
}

local profileStore = ProfileService.GetProfileStore(
	"player_main",
	defaultPlayerData
)

local message = {
	["sessionDetected"] = "\nSame account is currently launched on a different server.",
	["cantFetch"] = "\nData could not fetch, please rejoin. If this keeps occuring please contact our developers for support.",
	["wiped"] = "\nYour data has been wiped, please rejoin"
}

local cachedProfiles = {}

local function userAndIdString(player)
	return player.Name .. ":" .. player.userId
end

local function setupUserInfo(player)
	local profile = cachedProfiles[player]
	
	for name,data in pairs(profile.Data.stats) do
		local instance = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats"):FindFirstChild(name) or Instance.new(typeToInstance[typeof(data)])
		instance.Value = data
		instance.Name = name
		instance.Parent = player:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
	end
	
	--[[for name,data in pairs(profile.Data.data) do
		if typeof(data) == "table" then
			for name2,data2 in pairs(data) do
				local instance = Instance.new(typeToInstance[typeof(data2)])
				instance.Value = data2
				instance.Name = name2
				instance.Parent = player:WaitForChild("data"):WaitForChild(name)
			end
		else
			local instance = Instance.new(typeToInstance[typeof(data)])
			instance.Value = data
			instance.Name = name
			instance.Parent = player:WaitForChild("data")
		end
	end]]
	
	return true
end

local function playerAdded(player)
	if config.dataWipeEnabled then
		profileStore:WipeProfileAsync("player:" .. player.userId)
		warn("all players data in this session has been wiped, make sure to disable data wipe in data manager config.")
		return player:Kick(message.wiped)
	end
	
	local startTime = os.clock()
	local function fetch()
		local profile = profileStore:LoadProfileAsync("player:" .. player.userId, "ForceLoad")

		if profile then
			profile:Reconcile()
			profile:ListenToRelease(function()
				cachedProfiles[player] = nil
				player:Kick(message.sessionDetected)
			end)

			if player:IsDescendantOf(Players) then
				cachedProfiles[player] = profile
				return profile
			else
				profile:Release()
				warn(userAndIdString(player) .. "'s userdata could not fetch.")
			end
		else
			player:Kick(message.cantFetch)
		end
	end
	
	local profile = fetch()
	setupUserInfo(player)
	
	warn(userAndIdString(player) .. " userdata has been loaded, took " .. os.clock() - startTime .. " ms.")
	TableUtil.Print(profile.Data, "data", true, true)
	print("------------------------")
	
	task.wait(1)
	player.dataLoaded.Value = true
end

local function playerRemoving(player)
	local profile = cachedProfiles[player]
	if profile then
		local startTime = os.clock()
		task.spawn(function()
			print("------------------------")
			TableUtil.Print(profile.Data, userAndIdString(player) .. "'s data before release", true, true)
		end)
		profile:Release()
		warn(userAndIdString(player) .. " userdata has been released, took " .. os.clock() - startTime .. " ms.")
	end
end

local manager = {}

function manager:Init()
	print("Data manager has been initialized on this server.")
	for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		task.spawn(function()
			playerAdded(player)
		end)
	end

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(playerAdded)
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(playerRemoving)
end

function manager:GetProfile(player)
	local profile = cachedProfiles[player]
	
	if profile then
		return profile
	end
	
	return false
end

function manager:GetOfflineProfile(userId)
	return profileStore:LoadProfileAsync("player:" .. userId)
end

return manager  -  Editar

  03:10:08.309  -- local Madwork = _G.Madwork
--[[
{Madwork}

-[ProfileService]---------------------------------------
	(STANDALONE VERSION)
	DataStore profiles - universal session-locked savable table API
	
	Official documentation:
		https://madstudioroblox.github.io/ProfileService/

	DevForum discussion:
		https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ProfileService/667805
	
	WARNINGS FOR "Profile.Data" VALUES:
	 	! Do not create numeric tables with gaps - attempting to replicate such tables will result in an error;
		! Do not create mixed tables (some values indexed by number and others by string key), as only
		     the data indexed by number will be replicated.
		! Do not index tables by anything other than numbers and strings.
		! Do not reference Roblox Instances
		! Do not reference userdata (Vector3, Color3, CFrame...) - Serialize userdata before referencing
		! Do not reference functions
		
	WARNING: Calling ProfileStore:LoadProfileAsync() with a "profile_key" which wasn't released in the SAME SESSION will result
		in an error! If you want to "ProfileStore:LoadProfileAsync()" instead of using the already loaded profile, :Release()
		the old Profile object.
		
	Members:
	
		ProfileService.ServiceLocked         [bool]
		
		ProfileService.IssueSignal           [ScriptSignal] (error_message, profile_store_name, profile_key)
		ProfileService.CorruptionSignal      [ScriptSignal] (profile_store_name, profile_key)
		ProfileService.CriticalStateSignal   [ScriptSignal] (is_critical_state)
	
	Functions:
	
		ProfileService.GetProfileStore(profile_store_index, profile_template) --> [ProfileStore]
			profile_store_index   [string] -- DataStore name
			OR
			profile_store_index   [table]: -- Allows the developer to define more GlobalDataStore variables
				{
					Name = "StoreName", -- [string] -- DataStore name
					-- Optional arguments:
					Scope = "StoreScope", -- [string] -- DataStore scope
				}
			profile_template      [table] -- Profiles will default to given table (hard-copy) when no data was saved previously

		ProfileService.IsLive() --> [bool] -- (CAN YIELD!!!)
			-- Returns true if ProfileService is connected to live Roblox DataStores
				
	Members [ProfileStore]:
	
		ProfileStore.Mock   [ProfileStore] -- Reflection of ProfileStore methods, but the methods will use a mock DataStore
		
	Methods [ProfileStore]:
	
		ProfileStore:LoadProfileAsync(profile_key, not_released_handler) --> [Profile] or nil -- not_released_handler(place_id, game_job_id)
			profile_key            [string] -- DataStore key
			not_released_handler   nil or []: -- Defaults to "ForceLoad"
				[string] "ForceLoad" -- Force loads profile on first call
				OR
				[string] "Steal" -- Steals the profile ignoring it's session lock
				OR
				[function] (place_id, game_job_id) --> [string] "Repeat", "Cancel", "ForceLoad" or "Steal"
					place_id      [number] or nil
					game_job_id   [string] or nil

				-- not_released_handler [function] will be triggered in cases where the profile is not released by a session. This
				--	function may yield for as long as desirable and must return one of three string values:

						["Repeat"] - ProfileService will repeat the profile loading proccess and may trigger the release handler again
						["Cancel"] - ProfileStore:LoadProfileAsync() will immediately return nil
						["ForceLoad"] - ProfileService will repeat the profile loading call, but will return Profile object afterwards
							and release the profile for another session that has loaded the profile
						["Steal"] - The profile will usually be loaded immediately, ignoring an existing remote session lock and applying
							a session lock for this session.

		ProfileStore:GlobalUpdateProfileAsync(profile_key, update_handler) --> [GlobalUpdates] or nil
			-- Returns GlobalUpdates object if update was successful, otherwise returns nil
			profile_key      [string] -- DataStore key
			update_handler   [function] (global_updates [GlobalUpdates])
			
		ProfileStore:ViewProfileAsync(profile_key, version) --> [Profile] or nil
			-- Reads profile without requesting a session lock; Data will not be saved and profile doesn't need to be released
			profile_key   [string] -- DataStore key
			version       nil or [string] -- DataStore key version

		ProfileStore:ProfileVersionQuery(profile_key, sort_direction, min_date, max_date) --> [ProfileVersionQuery]
			profile_key      [string]
			sort_direction   nil or [Enum.SortDirection]
			min_date         nil or [DateTime]
			max_date         nil or [DateTime]
			
		ProfileStore:WipeProfileAsync(profile_key) --> is_wipe_successful [bool]
			-- Completely wipes out profile data from the DataStore / mock DataStore with no way to recover it.
						
		* Parameter description for "ProfileStore:GlobalUpdateProfileAsync()":
		
			profile_key      [string] -- DataStore key
			update_handler   [function] (GlobalUpdates) -- This function gains access to GlobalUpdates object methods
				(update_handler can't yield)

	Methods [ProfileVersionQuery]:

		ProfileVersionQuery:NextAsync() --> [Profile] or nil -- (Yields)
			-- Returned profile has the same rules as profile returned by :ViewProfileAsync()
		
	Members [Profile]:
	
		Profile.Data              [table] -- Writable table that gets saved automatically and once the profile is released
		Profile.MetaData          [table] (Read-only) -- Information about this profile
		
			Profile.MetaData.ProfileCreateTime   [number] (Read-only) -- os.time() timestamp of profile creation
			Profile.MetaData.SessionLoadCount    [number] (Read-only) -- Amount of times the profile was loaded
			Profile.MetaData.ActiveSession       [table] (Read-only) {place_id, game_job_id} / nil -- Set to a session link if a
				game session is currently having this profile loaded; nil if released
			Profile.MetaData.MetaTags            [table] {["tag_name"] = tag_value, ...} -- Saved and auto-saved just like Profile.Data
			Profile.MetaData.MetaTagsLatest      [table] (Read-only) -- Latest version of MetaData.MetaTags that was definetly saved to DataStore
				(You can use Profile.MetaData.MetaTagsLatest for product purchase save confirmation, but create a system to clear old tags after
				they pile up)

		Profile.MetaTagsUpdated   [ScriptSignal] (meta_tags_latest) -- Fires after every auto-save, after
			--	Profile.MetaData.MetaTagsLatest has been updated with the version that's guaranteed to be saved;
			--  .MetaTagsUpdated will fire regardless of whether .MetaTagsLatest changed after update;
			--	.MetaTagsUpdated may fire after the Profile is released - changes to Profile.Data are not saved
			--	after release.

		Profile.RobloxMetaData    [table] -- Writable table that gets saved automatically and once the profile is released
		Profile.UserIds           [table] -- (Read-only) -- {user_id [number], ...} -- User ids associated with this profile

		Profile.KeyInfo           [DataStoreKeyInfo]
		Profile.KeyInfoUpdated    [ScriptSignal] (key_info [DataStoreKeyInfo])
		
		Profile.GlobalUpdates     [GlobalUpdates]
		
	Methods [Profile]:
	
		-- SAFE METHODS - Will not error after profile expires:
		Profile:IsActive() --> [bool] -- Returns true while the profile is active and can be written to
			
		Profile:GetMetaTag(tag_name) --> value [any]
			tag_name   [string]
		
		Profile:Reconcile() -- Fills in missing (nil) [string_key] = [value] pairs to the Profile.Data structure
		
		Profile:ListenToRelease(listener) --> [ScriptConnection] (place_id / nil, game_job_id / nil)
			-- WARNING: Profiles can be released externally if another session force-loads
			--	this profile - use :ListenToRelease() to handle player leaving cleanup.
			
		Profile:Release() -- Call after the session has finished working with this profile
			e.g., after the player leaves (Profile object will become expired) (Does not yield)

		Profile:ListenToHopReady(listener) --> [ScriptConnection] () -- Passed listener will be executed after the releasing UpdateAsync call finishes;
			--	Wrap universe teleport requests with this method AFTER releasing the profile to improve session lock sharing between universe places;
			--  :ListenToHopReady() will usually call the listener in around a second, but may ocassionally take up to 7 seconds when a release happens
			--	next to an auto-update in regular usage scenarios.

		Profile:AddUserId(user_id) -- Associates user_id with profile (GDPR compliance)
			user_id   [number]

		Profile:RemoveUserId(user_id) -- Unassociates user_id with profile (safe function)
			user_id   [number]

		Profile:Identify() --> [string] -- Returns a string containing DataStore name, scope and key; Used for debug;
			-- Example return: "[Store:"GameData";Scope:"Live";Key:"Player_2312310"]"
		
		Profile:SetMetaTag(tag_name, value) -- Equivalent of Profile.MetaData.MetaTags[tag_name] = value
			tag_name   [string]
			value      [any]
		
		Profile:Save() -- Call to quickly progress global update state or to speed up save validation processes (Does not yield)

		-- VIEW-MODE ONLY:

		Profile:ClearGlobalUpdates() -- Clears all global updates data from a profile payload

		Profile:OverwriteAsync() -- (Yields) Saves the profile payload to the DataStore and removes the session lock
		
	Methods [GlobalUpdates]:
	
	-- ALWAYS PUBLIC:
		GlobalUpdates:GetActiveUpdates() --> [table] {{update_id, update_data [table]}, ...}
		GlobalUpdates:GetLockedUpdates() --> [table] {{update_id, update_data [table]}, ...}
		
	-- ONLY WHEN FROM "Profile.GlobalUpdates":
		GlobalUpdates:ListenToNewActiveUpdate(listener) --> [ScriptConnection] (update_id, update_data)
			update_data   [table]
		GlobalUpdates:ListenToNewLockedUpdate(listener) --> [ScriptConnection] (update_id, update_data)
			update_data   [table]
		GlobalUpdates:LockActiveUpdate(update_id)  -- WARNING: will error after profile expires
		GlobalUpdates:ClearLockedUpdate(update_id) -- WARNING: will error after profile expires
